### Sections (DOM order; `data-group`, default state, persisted collapse key)

Every section is made by `makeSection(title, group, collapsed)` (js/20-mixer-layout.js:9302–9357): `.sidebar-section[data-group]` > `.section-header[title]` > `.section-title[role=button][tabindex=0][aria-expanded]` + `.section-chevron`, then `.section-body`. Collapse state persists under `settingsManager 'sidebar.sections'` keyed by **title text** (9201–9210); Settings → Interface hides a section as `.ui-hidden` under `'ui.hiddenSections'` key `section:<title>` (js/43). Group hue: `[data-group="core"] { --sec-h: 242; --sec-c: 0.16 }`, `expressive` 190 / 0.15, `system` 250 / 0.08 (css/01-buttons.css:43–45); title gradient slices by inline `--rows` / `--i` (js/20:2657–2669, js/43:280–296; css/21-sidebar.css:164–190).

| # | Side | Title | `data-group` | Default | Right-bar `--rows` / `--i` | Simple layout | Position selector (no id; see the note under the table) | Slice |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | left | Brush | core | open | (left bar: stamped by js/43 `restampGroups` only) | kept | `#sidebar-left .lsb-body > .sidebar-section:nth-of-type(1)` | A2 L3 |
| 1 | left | Multi-Brush | core | collapsed | " | hidden (`SIMPLE_NEW_HIDDEN`, js/43:62) | `… .lsb-body > .sidebar-section:nth-of-type(2)` | A2 L4 |
| 2 | left | Stroke and replay | expressive | collapsed | " | hidden | `… .lsb-body > .sidebar-section:nth-of-type(3)` | A2 L5 |
| 3 | right | Swirl Together | core | collapsed | 5 / 0 | kept | `#sidebar-right > :nth-child(1 of .sidebar-section)` (= `.sidebar-section:nth-of-type(2)` at HEAD) | A3 |
| 4 | right | Simulation | core | collapsed | 5 / 1 | hidden | `:nth-child(2 of .sidebar-section)` (= `nth-of-type(3)`) | A3 |
| 5 | right | Colors and palettes | core | collapsed | 5 / 2 | hidden | `:nth-child(3 of .sidebar-section)` (= `nth-of-type(4)`) | A4 |

| 16 | right | Settings | system | collapsed | 4 / 3 | pinned (never hideable; `PINNED`, js/43:67) | `:nth-child(14 of .sidebar-section)` (= `nth-of-type(15)`) | A8 |

**Position selectors (column added after the critique).** `#sidebar-right`'s **first child is `.rsb-rail`**, not a section: `buildRightFold` does `bar.insertBefore(rail, bar.firstChild)` (js/20:2584; called from js/20:87 after `buildSidebar`). So `#sidebar-right > .sidebar-section:nth-of-type(n)` needs n = section index + 1 (Swirl Together 2 … Settings 15), and a compact window shifts it once more (js/13-compact-mode.js:56–62 moves `#mixer-strip` into the bar right after the rail). The slice tables originally used `nth-of-type` with the bare index (A3–A5, one too low) or the dump's `domOrder` (A6–A7, one too high); every one of those ten selectors matched the wrong section and has been rewritten to `#sidebar-right > :nth-child(k of .sidebar-section)` with k = the 1-based right-bar index above, a form that ignores non-section siblings (`:nth-child(An+B of S)`: Chrome 111+, so Chrome 155 and Electron 39). The left bar needs no such care: `.lsb-body` holds only the three sections (js/20:2411–2413), so `.lsb-body > .sidebar-section:nth-of-type(n)` is right as written. Mutate shader and Layers have hooks of their own (`#mutation-section` js/20:2917, `.section-layers`). The title text remains the only script-level key (soft contract, X2); whether prep step 4 should add a per-section attribute instead is open question 1 in `prep/work/open-items/A-critic.md`. Workstream E must take section selectors from this column, not from the slice headings as first written.

## How to read the tables

Every control table has the brief's nine columns, in this order (the header line is normalised to the short form):

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| # | the slice's row id (A1 `1.2.3`, A2 `L3.8`, A3 `P3` / `M1` / plain numbers, A4 plain numbers, A5 `M` / `E` / `L` / `S` / `G` / `H` / `D` / `P` / `B` / `R` prefixes, A6 `A` / `B` / `K` / `D`, A7 `T` / `F` / `D`, A8 plain + `E` / `S` / `U`, A9 per-table numbers with `c` for chrome). Cross-references inside a slice ("see Open questions 4", "open question 1") refer to that slice's own list, reproduced unchanged under its heading in the combined Open questions. |
| Control (as labelled) | the visible or accessible name as the dump reports it |
| Selector | id, or a stable `parent .class:nth-of-type(n)` path where there is none |
| Element / role | tag, type, computed role (dump `cdp`) or page-side fallback (`fallback`) |
| State attrs | `aria-*`, class toggles, `:disabled`, `.checked`, inline `style` state, with the file:line that writes them |
| Setting key | registry (`js/01a-param-registry.js` SLIDERS / CHECKBOXES / SELECTS → `slider.<id>` etc. on Save), `settingsManager` key, `config.*`, `localStorage`, or none |
| Syncs? | shared (in the room look, js/06b) / personal (dropped by `dropPersonal`) / n/a, with the mechanism |
| State-change path | the handler that already runs when the control changes (for CSS prep step 5), and the incoming-remote handler for shared controls |
| Notes | markup source, CSS rules, dump order, oddities |

Dump-order notation differs per slice and was left as written: A1 "order 14", A2 "dump order 136", A3 "Dump order 215", A4 "dump 256" / "orders 256–270", A5 none (records filtered by section), A6 "orders 413–494", A7 "[497]", A8 "orders 600, 602", A9 "`o` 1". All refer to the `order` field of `controls.json` (or of the named lazy dump).

Shared vocabulary (cited in full inside A1 and A3): `reg` = registry; `SM` = `settingsManager`; `LS` = raw `localStorage`; send path for shared controls = document-capture `input` / `change` / `click` listener (js/06b:487–490) + 1 s poll (06b:494) → `sendRoomLook`; receive = `onRoomLook` → `applyRoomLookChange` (06b:551): sliders glide via `mpGlideTo` → `glideWrite` (06c:101–118, dispatches `input` then `change` under `window.__mpApplyingRemote`), checkboxes / selects / sections via `applyLookSections` → `applyPresetSnapshot` (js/12:1033); visual on arrival `flashControl` (06e:373–384) sets `--mp-peer` + `.mp-peer-touch` on the control's face (`.mixer-channel` / `.control-group` / `.checkbox-group`). Range inputs everywhere carry inline `--min` / `--max` / `--val` (js/06-slider-updater.js:9–24) and sit in a `.fader-stack` wrapper (06:67–110).

**`—` in a cell (legend added after the critique).** A `—` in Setting key, Syncs? or State-change path means **none / not applicable**: the row is a readout, label, container, heading, panel or lazy menu with no setting of its own, nothing of its own on the wire, and no handler of its own (it follows the control row it belongs to), or a plain command button with no setting. Read it as `none` in Setting key, `n/a` in Syncs? and `none (no handler of its own)` in State-change path. It never stands for "not researched": a row the slice could not verify says so in words ("not verified", "open question"). The critique counted 487 such cells in 56 of the 91 control tables (Setting key 255, Syncs? 147, State-change path 85); they were left as `—` rather than rewritten so the slice text stays as the slices wrote it.

## Not covered (union of the slices' own lists)

- A live room was not exercised; every "shared / incoming" column is read from js/06a–06e and js/12 (F5 owns the sync baseline). The connected view, both popovers and the room menu were dumped hidden.
- The Electron build (custom titlebar, window fade, Electron-only rows: Export → Output Folder, Settings → ComfyUI, `#openPresetsFolderBtn`, `#penWindowMouseReturn`, `#transparentRestartRow`, the close-ask dialog) is described from source; the dump is the web build.
- Not opened live: `#mixer-more-panel` contents (only below ~900 CSS px), the preset ⋯ menu, the startup fork, every `.delete-modal` except delete-palette, the SAM / depth download modals, the mask editor, the layer-transform overlay, text arrange, the take prompt, `#captureArea`, the eyedropper veil / loupe, `#hkBindBar`, `#hkWheel`, the toasts, the pen window's own document, the phone dialog `#phonePadModal` (js/54), the Tunnel scene's quick controls (read from their spec js/30:318–345).
- Layers: only the Sim row exists in the scratch profile; picture, paint, collider and path rows are written from `renderLayers` / `renderPathLayersUI`, not from a capture.
- Out of scope by the README: `phone/`, `scripts/copy-sink/index.html`, `interaction-design-concept/`, `Design system breakdown exploration/`, the no-WebGL fatal screen, `window.alert` / `window.confirm` dialogs.
- The study (`design/study-lite.html`) was not read by any A slice; mapping to study components is workstream E.
- `scripts/test/inventory/hotkeys.json` was not checked for default bindings beyond the key caps the dump shows.

## A1 — Top bar `#mixer-strip`

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A1-top-bar.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

**Scope.** The top bar as `buildMixerStrip` ships it (js/20-mixer-layout.js:805–879), left to right: the fader cells (`faderChannel` :1127, `attachPerceptualProxy` :1053), the material picker on the Fluid cell, the colour cell (`buildColorChannel` :1203), the right-hand stack (`buildPresetsChannel` :1733 over `buildActionsChannel` :1517), the More overflow cell (js/46-strip-more.js) and the dividers. Brush Size and Multi-Brush are built by the same `faderChannel` and then re-parented into `#sidebar-left`; they get a short cross-reference table here and belong to slice A2.

**Enumeration of record.** `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json`, generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00.663Z at HEAD `c4796da` (web build, headless Chrome 155, viewport 1584×805 CSS px, DPR 1, `--ui-scale` 1, scratch profile = app defaults, layout "full", nothing hidden by js/43). Surface `mixer-strip` holds 35 records (orders 14–48) plus `overlay:mixer-more-panel` (order 832). The Presets popup is `lazy-presets-popup.json` (18 records, opened by a trusted click on `#mixerPresetsTrigger`). Every row below names its dump `order` so the critic can diff.

**Counts.** controls: 26 strip-resident interactive controls in the dump + 18 in the Presets popup (lazy dump) + 8 lazy-not-in-dump (Presets panel rows that need user presets, a group, or the first-open fork) + 3 re-parented (Brush Size canonical + proxy, Multi-Brush slider; cross-referenced to A2) = **55 interactive**; 10 container records (8 `.mixer-channel`, 1 `role=group` switch, 1 `role=group` More panel). sections: **12 cells** (8 fader cells including the 2 re-parented ones, Color, Presets, Transport, More). Dividers: 2.

**Not covered.** The colour picker popover `.cp-pop` (opened from `#colorPicker`; `lazy-colour-picker.json`, 18 controls) and the per-arm menus: surface 4 (A4). The left-sidebar head that receives Brush Size (`.lsb-head`, Stabilization) and the Multi-Brush section body: A2. The quality underbar: A3. The More panel's parked state was **not opened live** (it only appears when the bar overflows, below ~900 px at scale 1; the dump saw it hidden). Preset ⋯ menus, group heads and the first-open fork exist only with user presets / a fresh profile and are listed from the builder, not the dump. Electron adds `#custom-titlebar` only; nothing in the strip differs.

### How to read the tables

Columns are the brief's nine, in order. Abbreviations: `reg` = `js/01a-param-registry.js` SLIDERS/CHECKBOXES/SELECTS; `Settings.*` = `js/11-settings-interface.js` (`saveSlider` :69, `loadSlider` :79, `saveCheckbox` :114, `loadCheckbox` :124, `saveSelect` :166) over `settingsManager` namespace `fluidUI:` (js/09:8); `SM` = `settingsManager.get/set`; `LS` = raw `localStorage`. "shared" = in the room look (js/06b-mp-look.js); "personal" = stripped by `dropPersonal` (06b:80–97) through `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL` (06b:55–68) or `isPersonalSlider` (06b:70–75, registry category `brush`/`audio`). Sync send path for every shared control: document-capture `input`/`change`/`click` listener (06b:487–490) + 1 s poll (06b:494) → `sendRoomLook` (06b:425) with `lookDelta` (06b:362). Receive: `onRoomLook` (06b:515) → `applyRoomLookChange` (06b:551): sliders glide via `mpGlideTo` (06c:156) → `glideWrite` (06c:101–118, sets `--val`, dispatches `input`, final `change`); checkboxes/selects/sections via `applyLookSections` → js/12 `applyPresetSnapshot` (:1033). Visual on arrival: `flashControl` (06e:373–384) sets `--mp-peer` + `.mp-peer-touch` on the control's **face** = its `.mixer-channel` (06e:366–370), styled at css/21-sidebar.css:1472–1480 (1.4 s keyframe, outline in the peer's colour). That face/`--mp-peer` pair is the existing code path for the theme's `--ga`/`--pc` on strip cells. "flash: cell" below means that path reaches this control.

Range inputs everywhere carry inline `--min`/`--max`/`--val` (js/06-slider-updater.js:9–24, on `input`/`change`), which css/slider-styles.css:17–28 turns into `--pos` for the fill. Every setter that writes `.value` programmatically also writes `--val` (05h, 05e, 29, 06c). `aria-valuetext` on a fader is kept equal to its `.ch-value` text by a `MutationObserver` (`nameFader`, js/20:1040–1051).

### 4. Per-cell metadata (the strip's equivalent of section data-group / collapse / chrome)

The strip has one `data-group="core"` (js/20:808) and no collapse state. What varies per cell:

| Cell (left → right at boot) | `data-ui-key` → js/43 key | `data-ui-label` | `data-accent` | Hideable (43) / Simple keeps | Parkable (46) and park order | Room sync of its controls | Dump rect (x, w) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Brush Size (→ `#sidebar-left .lsb-head`) | `Brush Size` → not collected from the strip after the move (inferred, see 1.1.1); 43:56 "always on" | — | orange | — | no (moved before 46 builds) | personal | A2 |
| Material Type | `Fluid` → `strip:Fluid` | `Material Type` | blue | yes / hidden in Simple | yes, **last** out (46:69–72) | curl shared unless a material is on; material shared as a section | 4, 187 |
| Viscosity | `Viscosity` | — | purple | yes / hidden | yes, **2nd** (`PARK_FIRST`) | shared | 192, 153 |
| Isolation | `Isolation` | — | green | yes / hidden | yes, **1st** | shared | 346, 153 |
| Laminar / Blend | `Color Blend` | `Laminar / Blend` | orange | yes / hidden | yes, after Velocity and Density | shared | 499, 166 |
| Multi-Brush (→ left Multi-Brush section body) | `Multi-Brush` | — | yellow | section-level `section:Multi-Brush` (43:63) | no (never in the strip) | personal | A2 |
| Time | `Time` | — | pink | yes / hidden | yes, **3rd** | shared | 666, 153 |
| Density | `Density` | — | cyan | yes / hidden | yes (5th: from the right after Velocity) | shared | 820, 153 |
| Velocity | `Velocity` | — | cyan | yes / hidden | yes (4th) | shared | 973, 153 |
| More | none (46:247) | — | — | no | is the parking place | n/a | hidden at boot |
| divider | — | — | — | auto (43 `fixDividers`) | — | — | |
| Color | `Color` | — | pink | yes / **kept** | never | chips, swatch personal; Limit shared | 1135, 254 |
| divider | — | — | — | auto | — | — | |
| Presets | `Presets` | — | — | yes / **kept** | never | apply shared as values | 1406, 166 (trigger) |
| Transport | `Transport` | — | — | yes / **kept** | never | Clear shared as an event; Pause/Stop personal | 1406–1572 (row) |

Full park order from `computeParkOrder` (46:64–73) with the dump's candidate list [Fluid, Viscosity, Isolation, Color Blend, Time, Density, Velocity]: Isolation, Viscosity, Time, then Velocity, Density, Color Blend, Fluid; they return in reverse. Hiding is `.ui-hidden` = `display:none !important` (css/22:1940). Settings → Interface lists the cells as checkboxes with `data-ui-key="strip:<key>"` (43:442) labelled by `data-ui-label || key` (43:138).

### 5. State-change paths for CSS prep step 5 (where `--i`, `--ga`, `--gc`, `--pc` could ride)

| Control kind | Local change runs | Remote change runs (shared controls only) | What already changes visually | Candidate element for the runtime properties |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Plain fader (viscosity, velocityInfluence, velocityDissipation, curl) | the input's `input` listener (05h:550–619) and js/06-slider-updater `onInput` (:19–24, writes `--val`) | `glideWrite` (06c:101–118) dispatches the same `input` once per frame while gliding; `flashControl` (06e:373–384) sets `--mp-peer` + `.mp-peer-touch` on the `.mixer-channel` | `--val` fill, `.ch-value` text, `.mp-peer-touch` outline/keyframe (css/21:1472–1480) | the `input` (own position) and the `.mixer-channel` (peer glow: `--mp-peer` is already the per-player colour) |
| Curved fader (colorBlend, timeScale, densityDissipation; brushSize in A2) | proxy `input` (js/20:1079–1086) → canonical `input`; canonical `input`/`change` → `reflect` (1092–1106); 250 ms poll (1114–1119) | glide writes the **canonical**; `reflect` moves the proxy; flash on the cell | proxy `--val`, readout, `aria-valuetext` | the proxy `input` for brightness, the cell for peer glow |
| Material select | `change` → `setMode` (29:214–235); `mm-sync` custom event on silent resets (29:253) | `applyState` (29:285–303) via js/12:1820; **no flash** today | select `value`/width, slider `title`, readout text | the select / `.ch-material` cell; a remote path would need `onRoomLookChanged` to treat `material` as a flashable key (Open questions) |

## A2 — Left sidebar `#sidebar-left` (head, Brush, Multi-Brush, Stroke and replay, rail)

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A2-left-sidebar.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

**Dump of record:** `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json`, generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00.663Z at HEAD `c4796da` (Chrome 155 headless, 1584×805 CSS px, DPR 1, `--ui-scale` 1, scratch profile = defaults: left bar open, Brush open, Multi-Brush and Stroke and replay collapsed). Lazy UI: `prep/baseline/dom/lazy-arm-tip-menu.json` (7 records). Records in scope: the 149 with `surface: "sidebar-left"` plus the `.lsb-toggle` record under `surface: "main-area"` (order 198).

**Counts (from the dump, verified by script):** 150 records = **110 interactive controls** (including the 3 `.section-title` buttons), 33 `<label>`s, 5 containers (2 `.mixer-channel`, 3 `.sidebar-section`), 2 `radiogroup` wrappers. 28 interactive controls are visible at boot (defaults hide the Pressure block, Spacing, Hide guide, Replay Period, both ramps and the two collapsed sections). **Sections: 3.** Lazy, not in the dump: the arm-tip menu (7), brush-preset chips, custom-shape tiles (in four places), the shape context menu, the mirror "Turn off" rows, the Save ✓ state.

| Multi-Brush | 20 | 13 | 4 | 2 + 1 |

### Sync: what this sidebar shares with a room

The README calls the left bar personal. The code agrees for everything **except one slider**. Exact mechanism in `js/06b-mp-look.js`:

| Rule | Lines | Left-bar controls it covers |
|---|---|---|
| `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS` — dropped from checkboxes/selects on send and receive (86-89) and never captured (184-188) | 06b:40-46 | `cursorToggle`, `brushEraser` |
| `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL.sliderCategories = { brush, audio }` → `isPersonalSlider(id)` reads `ParamRegistry.SLIDERS[id].category` | 06b:55-56, 70-75; dropped 83-85 | `brushSize` (01a:110), `multiplier` (01a:111), `brushSteady` (172), `brushSpacing` (173), `brushFlow` (175), `brushDabInterval` (176), `brushJitter` (177), `brushTipTexture` (178); also `brushStabilizer` 171 and `brushHardness` 174 (registry only, no control) |
| `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL.checkboxes` | 06b:57-58 | `symmetrySameAngle`, `symmetryFaceCenter` (and `randomColor`/`stepPalette`, which arm 0's Random/Palette mode mirrors) |
| `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL.selects` | 06b:59 | `symmetryMode`, `splatInMode`, `splatOutMode` |
| `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL.sections` deleted whole | 06b:67, 82 | `brushState`, `brushTip`, `armColors` (captured at 168, 170, 184 then dropped) |
| `dropPersonal` runs on the share copy (340) and inside `sanitizeLook` on receive (250); the receive allow-list `LOOK_ALLOW` has no brushState/brushTip/armColors key at all | 06b:80-94, 217-219, 250, 340 | everything above, both directions |
| Not in the look at all: `captureLookSnapshot` copies only registry sliders/checkboxes/selects plus the named sections | 06b:146-188 | every settingsManager-only key here (`brush.target`, `brush.velOnly/velMode`, `brush.splatMode`, `brush.tip`, `brush.presets`, `brush.replay*`, `brush.splat*Dist/Ms`, `ui.brush.tipOpen`, `input.buttonModes`, `hotkeys.strokeLocks`, `strokeLock.showGuides`) and the two `localStorage` ghost keys |
| **Shared:** `symmetryHold` is registry category `simulation`, `simSlider: true` (01a:80), in no personal list, captured by `capturePresetSnapshot` (js/12-save-load.js:677) → `look.sliders` → sent (06b:335-340) and applied by the glide (`mpGlideTo` js/06c-mp-glide.js:156 → `glideWrite` 101) under `window.__mpApplyingRemote` (06b:28) | 01a:80; 06b:70-75 (not matched); 06c:101,156 | **Hold symmetry** only |

The brush itself rides every dab on the wire (`brushWireFields` js/06d-mp-paint-wire.js:77-90: tip, angle, velocity-only…), which is why none of it needs to sync. `multiplier` is also named in `MP_NO_GLIDE` (06c:40) as a guard for looks from other builds; it never arrives on this build.

### Not covered

- The 18 dump records under `surface: "overlay:.arm-colors-panel"` are the **top bar's Presets panel** (`.arm-colors-panel.mixer-presets-panel`, `mixer-preset-btn`, `#mixerPresetNameInput`…), not this sidebar; they share the `.arm-colors-panel` skin only. They belong to the A1 (top bar) slice.
- The right bar's fold tab `.rsb-toggle` (dump order 208, `buildRightFold` js/20:2530-2608) — A3.
- The shared colour picker popover `.cp-pop` that `.arm-picker` opens (js/64; dumped from the top bar well in `lazy-colour-picker.json`) — A1/A4.
- Destinations reached from here but built elsewhere: the mask editor (＋ / Clipboard import, js/33), `appConfirm` (Delete shape), the Radial Menu editor in Settings (js/58), the Layers panel's collider layer (Collider) — A3/A4.
- Compact-window behaviour (js/13 folds this bar) is cited, not inventoried. The pen window is out of scope.
- CSS values (workstream C), the selector contract (B) and the study mapping (E, deferred: `design/study-lite.html` exists but mapping is E's job).
- `prefers-reduced-motion` / `forced-colors` behaviour of these controls: none specific to this sidebar was found beyond the three global blocks the README lists.

## Right sidebar shell (added by the merge; precedes A3)

No slice owned the right bar's own chrome: the container, the folded rail's four buttons (dump orders 209–212, surface `sidebar-right`, section `null`) and the resize handle. A2 and A4 named them "another slice", A8 "right-bar shell". Rows below are read from `buildRightFold` (js/20-mixer-layout.js:2540–2609), `buildSidebar` (2611–2649) and `initSidebarResize` (207–235); the fold tab itself is A9 §6 row 2 (`.rsb-toggle`, dump 208). The critic should verify these against the dump.

## A3 — Right sidebar 1: Swirl Together, Simulation

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A3-right-1.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

Workstream A (inventory), slice A3-right-1. Written 2026-10-17 against the DOM dump `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json` (generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00Z, HEAD `c4796da`, headless Chrome 155, viewport 1584×805 CSS px, DPR 1, `--ui-scale` 1, every section opened by trusted Shift-click). The dump is the enumeration of record; every row below is one of its records or a lazily created element cited from the builder.

### Scope and counts

| | |
|---|---|
| Sections | 2: **Swirl Together** (right bar DOM order 3, first section) and **Simulation** (order 4). Both `data-group="core"`, both collapsed at boot. |
| Controls | **35 interactive**: Swirl Together 13 in-panel (incl. 3 live regions and 1 hidden legacy checkbox) + 7 in the Invite popover `#mpInvitePop` + 2 in the ⋯ menu `#mpRoomMenu`; Simulation 13 (1 select, 10 sliders, 2 checkboxes). Labels (13 in Simulation) are folded into their control's row, not counted. |
| Popovers | 2, body-mounted (`#mpInvitePop` dialog, `#mpRoomMenu` menu), present in the DOM hidden at boot (dump surfaces `overlay:mpInvitePop` 9 records, `overlay:mpRoomMenu` 3). |
| Chrome rows | per section: container, header, title button, chevron, body; Swirl Together adds the panel plate and 3 view containers; Simulation adds `#multigridPanel`. |
| Not covered | See the last section. |

**Scope correction.** The slice text named "gravity pad, resolution selects" under Simulation. In the shipping DOM they are not there: the gravity pad is in **Effects** (`#pressureConstant`, dump order 395, `.fx-row`; adopted by `buildEffectsSection`, js/20-mixer-layout.js:4181–4196 comment and :4197 `wirePressureField`) and the resolution selects are in the **quality underbar** (`#visualResolution` / `#physicsResolution` behind `.qub-dd-btn` proxies, dump orders 822–830; js/20-mixer-layout.js:4159–4160 comment, `buildQualityUnderbar` :2675). Both are inventoried in the cross-reference appendix at the end so the merge agent can dedupe against the Effects and underbar slices; the full rows belong to those owners.

### Shared anatomy (read once)

| Piece | Where it is made | What CSS keys off |
|---|---|---|
| Section shell `.sidebar-section[data-group]` > `.section-header` > `.section-title[role=button][tabindex=0][aria-expanded]` + `.section-chevron` ; `.section-body` | `makeSection` js/20-mixer-layout.js:9302–9357 | `.collapsed` (grid 0fr, css/21-sidebar.css:51–65, body :120–140), `.snap-collapse` (:197), `.ui-hidden` (css/22-overlays.css:1940), `[data-group="core"]` → `--sec-h: 242; --sec-c: 0.16` (css/01-buttons.css:43), title colour/gradient (css/21-sidebar.css:173–191), inline `--rows` / `--i` per section (`stampGroupRows` js/20:2657–2668) |

---

### Section 1 — Swirl Together

Built by `buildMultiArtistSection` js/20-mixer-layout.js:8361–8388: `makeSection('Swirl Together', 'core', true)` (:8365), then the static `#multiArtistPanel` (index.html:1390–1480) and the hidden `#multiplayerToggle` (index.html:1389) are moved into the body (:8368–8373). Wiring lives in js/06e-mp-panel.js (`initMultiplayerUI` :887–965), room lifecycle in js/06a-mp-core.js, the shared look in js/06b-mp-look.js, the phone door in js/54-phone-pads.js, the room report in js/61-room-report.js.

#### Invite popover — `#mpInvitePop` (lazy-mounted on `<body>`)

Static markup index.html:1445–1472 inside `#mpConnected`; `mountRoomPops` js/06e:771–776 moves it onto `<body>` once (never rebuilt: its ids are read by `renderShareMode`, js/44 tours :518, :531, js/54). Root: `div#mpInvitePop.brush-shape-menu.mp-pop[role=dialog][aria-label="Invite"]`, `hidden` attr toggled (js/06e:801, :824), `data-group` copied from the opener's section so buttons tint (`placeRoomPop` :778–780), inline `left`/`top` (:781–791, flips above when no room below), skin `.brush-shape-menu` css/20-mixer-strip.css:1354–1366 + `.mp-pop` css/21-sidebar.css:1300–1303 (252 px wide). Focus: `DialogFocus.popup` js/00b-dialog-focus.js:190 (:807). Closes on outside `mousedown`, Escape (stops propagation so 05n's Escape does not fire), resize, or when the sidebar scroll carries the button off screen (:811–814, :840–862). Clicks on `[data-pop-close]` close it; Code/QR/Hide and the copies keep it open (:875–879). Dump surface `overlay:mpInvitePop` (orders 833–841), not shown as a room would show it (F0 caveat).

### Section 2 — Simulation

Built by `buildSimulationSection` js/20-mixer-layout.js:4156–4179: `makeSection('Simulation', 'core', true)` (:4157), then `moveControlGroup` / `moveCheckboxGroup` (:9365–9387; each clears the group's inline `cssText`) pulls the static rows out of the orphan `.controls` panel in this order: `fpsCap`, `pressureDissipation`, `pressureIteration`, `wetInfluence`, `wetDrying`, `grainCleanup`, `macCormackToggle`, `multigridToggle`, then `#multigridPanel` whole (:4161–4177). Dump orders 229–255 confirm that order. Image Sharpness and Motion Detail are **not** here (quality underbar, :4159–4160).

Dump order 228, rect 274×714 open. |

#### Controls

All sliders: `input[type=range]` / **slider**, AX name = the label text with its readout ("PRESSURE DISSIPATION 0.950", uppercase from CSS), `--val`/`--min`/`--max` inline from js/06-slider-updater.js, native `:focus-visible` ring css/slider-styles.css:76–87. "Shared" = in a room the value travels in `look.sliders` (captured by `capturePresetSnapshot` js/12-save-load.js:162–164 from the registry's `SLIDERS`, filtered by `dropPersonal` js/06b-mp-look.js:80–96: category `brush`/`audio` and `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS` :39–45 are personal; `simulation` is not) and lands through `mpGlideTo` js/06c-mp-glide.js:156–177 → `glideWrite` :101–119 (sets `value`, `--val`, dispatches `input` then `change` with `window.__mpApplyingRemote` / `isProcessingRemoteEvent` set), or `mpSetSliderNow` :140–150 for `MP_NO_GLIDE` ids (:40). Persistence: `settingsManager 'slider.<id>'` written by `Settings.saveSliders` (js/12:196 → js/11-settings-interface.js:70) on the save path and restored by `loadSavedSliderValues` js/05h-slider-bindings.js:640–660 (writes `value`, `--val`, config and the readout).

Not interactive, not in the dump, but in the body: `#paletteCarousel.palette-carousel` (the trigger's host, emptied and rebuilt by `refreshPaletteCarousel`), `.palette-active` wrapper. Hotkeys that drive this section without touching it: Ctrl+←/→ step the palette colour (05n:589–597 `stepPaletteColour`), Shift+S / Shift+X add/remove the brush colour (05n:101–103).

### Surface: `#sidebar-right` › Layers

Body contents, in order (js/20:3617–3781): toolbar, hidden collider file input, `#layersPanel`, Path Layers subsection, "Capture & Preview" options group, `#imageUpload`, `#previewToggle`. The task text names `[data-cs]` collision controls; **no `data-cs` attribute exists anywhere in js/, css/ or index.html**. The collision hooks are `data-collision="1"` (row), `data-collision-layer` (controls block), `data-cinv`, `data-cref` (05k:409, 446, 456, 470).

#### Rows the theme must style but the dump never showed

Because the scratch profile has no project, the dump holds one row (Sim). A screenshot baseline for this section needs a fixture with: one picture layer (collapsed and open), one paint layer (`[data-raster]`, `.raster-active`), one depth-cut collider (`[data-collision]` with Threshold/Invert/Refresh), one painted collider ("Edit Collider"), one path layer with a path, and the drag state (`#layersPanel.layers-dragging`, `.drag-over`). Rows 36–61 above are written from `renderLayers` / `renderPathLayersUI`, not from a capture.

#### CSS prep step 5 — where `--i` / `--ga` / `--gc` / `--pc` could be set, per control (this slice)

| Control | Local change already passes through | Remote change already passes through |
|---|---|---|
| `#paletteSelect`, chips, `#paletteAddChip` | `applyPalette` 01:1010 → `refreshPaletteCarousel` 01:431 + `renderPalettePreview` 01:879 (both rebuild their nodes, so any property must be set inside those two functions, not on the old nodes); `updatePaletteStepIndicator` 01:971 for the active chip | `applyRoomLookChange` 06b:548 → `applyLookSections` 06b:258 → `applyPresetSnapshot` 12:1198 → the same two renderers, with `window.__mpApplyingRemote === true` (06b:263) and the sender in `onRoomLookChanged(change, fromId)` 06e:386 — the only place the peer colour (`cursorColour(fromId)` 06e:146) meets the change |
| Layer rows (every control) | `renderLayers` 05k:254 rebuilds the whole panel on any layer change | `applyPeerCollider` 06d:508–556 → `renderLayers`; peer rows are identifiable only by `layer.__peerOwner` (06d:249) |
| `#hoverCaptureToggle`, `#detachCaptureToggle` | their own `change` handlers (04f:291, 04g:545) | never (personal) |
| Section title | `toggleSection` 9224 (class flip) | `openSidebarSection` 9293 (same) |

`flashControl` (06e:373–384) is the existing remote-touch hook: it sets `--mp-peer` and `.mp-peer-touch` on `controlFace(id)` = nearest `.mixer-channel` / `.control-group` / `.checkbox-group` of `getElementById(id)` (06e:367–371; css/21-sidebar.css:1472–1481). It only fires for registry ids in `change.sliders/checkboxes/selects`, so nothing in this slice receives it today (palettes are look sections, layers are not in the look).

Slice of workstream A (brief `prep/briefs/A-inventory.md`). Enumeration of record: `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json` (generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00Z at HEAD `c4796da`, headless Chrome 155, viewport 1584×805, `--ui-scale` 1, every section opened by trusted Shift-click). Records filtered on `surface === 'sidebar-right'` and `section` in {`Mutate shader`, `Effects`}; the lazy dumps (`lazy-*.json`) hold nothing from these two sections.

| | Mutate shader | Effects | Total |
|---|---|---|---|

| of which: section container / title button | 1 / 1 | 1 / 1 | 2 / 2 |
| of which: `label` records (LabelText) | 9 | 41 | 50 |

Effects' 53 = 9 preview thumbs (`role=img`, focusable), 11 checkboxes, 7 selects, 22 sliders, 3 buttons, 1 `role=application` canvas. Mutate's 14 = 2 segmented buttons, 1 hidden mirror select, 1 slider, 1 select, 4 buttons, 5 lock checkboxes.

**Builders:** `buildMutationSection` js/20-mixer-layout.js:2915–3075, `wireMutationUI` 20:3077–3615, `buildEffectsSection` 20:4455–4522, `fxRow` 20:4413–4453, `fxTipBuild/Show/Hide` 20:4352–4405, `FX_INFO` 20:4329–4348, `wirePressureField` 20:4197–4322, `makeSection` 20:9302–9356. Static markup moved from `index.html`: shading 812–840, glow/scatter 842–869, gravity 1118–1137, border 1141–1151, breathing 1152–1180, dither 1185–1230, light source 1236–1278, light shift 1279–1331. Modules: js/05e-effect-controls.js (glow, scatter, shading, border bindings), js/05h-slider-bindings.js (generic registry→config binding: `sharpness`, `vibrance`), js/13-light-source.js, js/14-light-shift.js, js/68-dither.js, js/45-breathing.js, js/25-mutation-engine.js, js/57-mutation-thumbs.js, js/06-slider-updater.js (`.fader-stack` wrapper + `--val`).

**Not covered:** the pixel content of the baked previews (`assets/effects/<key>.png|gif`, 9 pairs); the `#breathCue` overlay (body-mounted by 45:255–259, belongs to the "everything else" surface); `#lightShiftWheel` drawing internals (14:130–560); js/25 engine and js/57 paint internals (no DOM beyond what is listed); the hotkey binder (js/49) and its badges on these controls; CSS values (workstream C). No headless Chrome was booted for this slice: everything is the dump plus source.

### Conventions used in the tables

- **Selector**: ids where they exist; otherwise `class` + position, marked "(nth)". The Effects section has **no id**: it is `#sidebar-right > :nth-child(6 of .sidebar-section)` (= `.sidebar-section:nth-of-type(7)` at HEAD, `.rsb-rail` first, js/20:2584; corrected after the critique) (title "Effects", `data-group="expressive"`); Mutate is `#mutation-section` (20:2917).
- **Setting key**: `registry SLIDERS/CHECKBOXES/SELECTS.<id>` (js/01a-param-registry.js) means the value rides presets, look links and the Save-settings snapshot (`Settings.saveSliders/saveCheckboxes/saveSelect`, js/12-save-load.js:195–200; capture list `SLIDER_IDS` 12:131, 12:163; restore `applyFromSettings` 12:267). `settingsManager <key>` means the module also writes that key on change. `config.X` is the live value the handler writes. "none" = session-only DOM state.
- **Syncs?**: *shared* = in the room look (`captureLookSnapshot` 06b:144–191 → `dropPersonal` 06b:79–96 keeps it); *personal* = dropped by `dropPersonal` (`isPersonalSlider` 06b:71–75: registry category `brush`/`audio`, or `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS` 06b:41–47); *n/a* = not captured at all. Incoming shared sliders arrive through `mpGlideTo` (06c:163–183): **glide** when the slider has ≥40 positions or a 1:1 `configKey` with decimals (06c:66–78), otherwise **step** (`mpSetSliderNow` 06c:148–158). Both end in `glideWrite` (06c:101–118), which sets the value, writes `--val`, dispatches `input` (and `change` when final) on the real element. Incoming checkboxes/selects go through `applyLookSections` (06b:257–313) → `applyPresetSnapshot` (12:1033) → `setCheck` 12:64 (dispatches `change`) / the select apply. So **every remote change re-enters the control's own DOM handler**, and `window.__mpRoomLookChanged(change, fromId, welcome)` (06b:598–599 → 06e:950 `onRoomLookChanged`) fires afterwards with the flat key list (`sliders.ridges`, `gravity`, …) and the sender: that is the one place a theme can set `--ga` / `--gc` / `--pc` for a shared control without a new loop.
- **State-change path**: the local DOM handler (what runs on `input`/`change`/`click`), then the remote entry when shared. Every sidebar range input also gets `--val`/`--min`/`--max` written on it by js/06-slider-updater.js (`setSliderVars` 06:10–18, `onInput` 06:20–25) and is wrapped in `.fader-stack` with a printed scale (`ensureStack` 06:67–109) unless `data-no-scale="1"`.

### Section: Effects (`#sidebar-right > :nth-child(6 of .sidebar-section)`; = `.sidebar-section:nth-of-type(7)` at HEAD, `.rsb-rail` first — the slice's `nth-of-type(6)` was Mutate, corrected after the critique; `data-group="expressive"`)

Order: 6th section. Every effect switch is an **fx row** built by `fxRow` (20:4413–4453): the existing `.control-group.checkbox-group` row gains `.fx-row` + `data-fx="<key>"`, loses its `title` (20:4424), has its label text normalised (20:4426), and gets a `span.fx-thumb` prepended. Panels keep the `.collapsible` class from index.html (no CSS rule matches `.collapsible`; visibility is inline `display` written by each module).

#### Effects — fx rows and preview thumbs (one row per effect; children listed in the panel tables below)

### State-change paths, summarised for CSS prep step 5

| Kind | Local path (already runs on change) | Remote path (already runs when a peer's value lands) | Where `--i` / `--ga` / `--gc` / `--pc` could be set without new loops |
|---|---|---|---|
| Registry sliders (all 22 in Effects) | the slider's `input` handler (05e / 05h / 13 / 14 / 68 / 45, cited per row) plus js/06-slider-updater `onInput` 06:20–25 (`--val`) | `glideWrite` 06c:101–118 dispatches `input` (and `change` when final) on the same element, with `window.__mpApplyingRemote === true` during the dispatch | one `input` listener (document capture, like 06b:486–488) can tell local from remote by `window.__mpApplyingRemote`; the sender id is only available in `window.__mpRoomLookChanged(change, fromId, welcome)` 06b:598 / 06e:950, keyed `sliders.<id>` |
| Registry checkboxes / selects (11 + 7) | the `change` handler cited per row | `setCheck` 12:64 / select apply inside `applyPresetSnapshot` 12:1033, under `window.__mpApplyingRemote` (06b:264) | same hook; keys `checkboxes.<id>` / `selects.<id>` |
| Pads (light position, light-shift path, gravity) | 13:159–216, 14:153–157, 20:4267–4299 (no DOM event; inline style writes) | `applyPresetSnapshot` 12:1236–1265 (light), `window.setGravityField` 20:4257 (gravity) | hook keys `lightPos`, `lightShiftPath`, `gravity` |
| Mutate's own controls (M2–M17) and the variant grid | click/input handlers cited above; nothing persists | none: not in the look (only the look a card applies is) | treat as personal; no remote glow applies |
| Section fold | `toggleSection` 20:9224–9297 → class → `aria-expanded` via MutationObserver 20:9343 | none (fold state is per screen) | `[aria-expanded]` on `.section-title` is already an ARIA-state hook |

## A6 — Right sidebar 4: Animations, Audio, Kaleidoscope (+ the Audio drawer `#recDrawer`)

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A6-right-4.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

**Scope.** Three sections of the right sidebar, in DOM order 9–11 of 14: **Animations** (`buildAnimationsSection` js/20-mixer-layout.js:3786–4131; the record strip `#recMini` from index.html:1560–1590, run by js/03-recording.js), **Audio** (`buildAudioSection` js/20:7205–7643, `ensureAudioDrawer` 7644–7650, `buildAudioDrawerControls` 7652–7933; engines js/22-audio-reactive.js, js/30-audio-scenes.js, js/40-audio-timing.js, js/40b-audio-cue-editor.js) and **Kaleidoscope** (`buildKaleidoscopeSection` js/20:4133–4154; `#mandalaPanel` index.html:933–978, `#kaleidoPanel` index.html:979–1026; js/05f-kaleido-controls.js, js/34-mandala-mode.js, js/63-kaleido-guides.js). Because the Audio section's "Full" mode is the body-level drawer `#recDrawer` (index.html:1599–1608, js/28-studio-drawer.js), that drawer is inventoried here too as a fourth table.

**Enumeration of record.** `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json`, generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00.663Z at HEAD `c4796da` (headless Chrome 155, 1584×805 CSS px, `--ui-scale` 1, every section opened by trusted Shift-click). Records used: `surface:"sidebar-right"` with `section` ∈ {Animations, Audio, Kaleidoscope} (orders 413–494) and `surface:"overlay:recDrawer"` (orders 741–795). None of the four `lazy-*.json` dumps touches this scope.

**Counts.** 137 dump records: 16 Animations, 18 Audio, 48 Kaleidoscope, 55 recDrawer. Of those, **89 are interactive controls** (12 + 13 + 23 + 41), 2 are `role=group` chip containers, 40 are `<label>`/LabelText rows and 6 are section chrome (3 `.sidebar-section` containers + 3 `.section-title` buttons). **Sections: 3** (all `data-group="expressive"`, all collapsed by default) plus the `#recDrawer` overlay. Lazy controls not in the dump are listed per table with "(lazy, not in dump)" and are not counted above.

**Not covered.** The 41 orphaned `overlay:.controls` records were not checked for duplicates of ids in this scope (the kaleido/mandala panels and `#recMini` are *moved*, not copied — js/20:4140–4151, 3793 — so none is expected). The Tunnel scene's quick controls were read from their spec (js/30:318–345), not rendered. `js/28-studio-drawer.js:38–49` (`close()`) was read only for the class it removes; whether closing the drawer also changes `#audioMode` is unverified (see Open questions). The `kaleido.runtime` persistence is cited but the 05h binding of registry sliders with `configKey:null` was not traced; the input handlers cited are the ones in js/05f that actually move the globals. No study mapping (that is workstream E).

**Conventions.** "Setting key" names the persisted key: `slider.<id>` / `checkbox.<id>` / `select.<id>` are written only on Save settings by js/12-save-load.js through js/11-settings-interface.js:70/115/167 (registry params); `settingsManager` keys are written immediately by the handler named. "Syncs?" follows js/06b-mp-look.js: a registry slider/checkbox/select is **shared** unless it is in `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS` (06b:39–45) or `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL` (06b:55–68: slider categories brush + audio, listed checkboxes/selects, and the whole `kaleido` runtime section); anything outside the registry is **n/a** (never in the look). Incoming shared sliders arrive through `applyRoomLookChange` (06b:548) → `mpGlideTo` (06c:156) → `glideWrite` (06c:101–116: sets `.value`, dispatches `input`, then `change` on the final frame, with `window.__mpApplyingRemote=true`); `kaleidoSegments` is in `MP_NO_GLIDE` (06c:40) and snaps via `mpSetSliderNow` (06c:140). Incoming checkboxes/selects arrive through `applyLookSections` (06b:258) → `applyPresetSnapshot` (12:1033, `_profileApplying=true` 12:1042) → `setVal` (12:11: `.value`, `dispatchEvent(change|input)`, `--val`). Presets (js/12) and Mutate reach the same controls by the same `setVal` path. Where a dump record has no id, the selector is given as `#parent .class:nth-of-type(n)` and marked "(nth)".

### Kaleidoscope (`#sidebar-right > :nth-child(9 of .sidebar-section)`; = `.sidebar-section:nth-of-type(10)` at HEAD — the slice's `nth-of-type(11)` was Text, corrected after the critique; 48 dump records, 23 interactive)

Body order (js/20:4140–4151): Mandala Overlay `.feature-head` row → `#mandalaPanel.collapsible.feature-body` (inline `display:none` until on) → Kaleido `.feature-head` row → `#kaleidoPanel.collapsible.feature-body` (hidden by `#kaleidoPanel{display:none}` until `.open`, css/styles.css:1141–1152). Both `.feature-head` rows are the switch-as-header-bar pattern (css/21-sidebar.css:1590–1668: the checkbox is drawn as a 30×16 toggle, `:checked` fills `var(--accent)`, `.feature-head:has(> input:checked)` squares the bottom corners and `.feature-head + .feature-body` hangs the panel from it).

Labels in this section (23 LabelText records) all follow the `label[for=<id>]` pattern except K5's group label "Fill" (no `for`), the three radio labels (wrapping) and K15's (no `for`). Every slider readout is a `span.value-display` inside the label, so the AX name of each slider includes its current value ("Facets 12", "Angle 0°"), which changes as the user drags — F3 should expect that.

### Audio drawer `#recDrawer` (body overlay, 55 dump records, 41 interactive)

Opened only by Audio Mode = Full (`applyAudioMode` js/20:7614–7620 → `studioDrawer.open('audio')` js/28:32–36 adds `.open`; `.rec-drawer{display:none}` / `.rec-drawer.open{display:flex}` css/styles.css:942–959). Static shell index.html:1599–1608 with `data-group="expressive"` and `data-active-tab="audio"` (one tab since the animation editor went, 2026-10-13); `#audioDrawerPanel` is filled at build time by `buildAudioDrawerControls` (js/20:7638, 7652–7933) so every id exists from boot. Height is set inline by the resize handle (js/28:74–77), capped at 60 vh ÷ `--ui-scale` (styles.css:950). Panel order: `.audio-construction-note` → `#audioDrawerEnableHost` → `#audioCueEditorHost` → `#audioDrawerLanes` → `details.audio-live-block`.

## A7 — Right sidebar 5: Text, Focus, Display

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A7-right-5.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

Workstream A slice. Scope: `#sidebar-right` sections **Text** (DOM order 12 of 14, `data-group="expressive"`), **Focus** (13, `system`) and **Display** (14, `system`), in that order. Builders: `buildTextSection` js/20-mixer-layout.js:6315–7185, `buildFocusSection` 7935–8020, `buildDisplaySection` 4541–4586; all three go through `makeSection` 9302–9357 and are appended by `buildSidebar` (Display at 2641). Display's controls are static markup from `index.html:1027–1116` (+ `#lockCanvasBorders` at 1332–1335) moved by `moveControlGroup` / `moveCheckboxGroup` (20:9365–9387, which also wipe the group's inline `style`); Text and Focus are built entirely in JS.

Enumeration of record: `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json`, generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00Z at HEAD `c4796da`, headless Chrome 155, 1584×805 CSS px, `--ui-scale` 1, web build (no Electron). Dump record numbers below are its `order` field, written `[n]`.

**Counts.** Dump records in scope: 89 (Text 47, Focus 11, Display 31) = 58 interactive controls + 3 section containers + 3 title buttons + 24 label records (`LabelText`) + 1 `role=group`. Interactive controls per section: Text 36, Focus 6, Display 16. Added from the builders because they are lazy or conditional and so absent from the dump: Text 11 kinds (list rows and their buttons, room rows, notes), Focus 1 (format readout), Display 4 (`.pen-screen` radios, `#penWindowMouseReturn`, `#penWindowHint`, `.pen-window-hint` notes). Sections: 3.

**Not covered.** The overlays these sections open live on other surfaces and are only cross-referenced: the text arrange chrome (`#text-arrange-canvas` in `#canvas-area`, `#text-arrange-bar` on `body`, js/23:776–801), `#focus-mode-hint` (dump [803], js/21:50–56), `#photoWarn` and the lazy `#photoSafeOffModal` alertdialog (js/05e:235ff), `#statsPanel`, the pen window itself (a second window, js/47:300ff), `#photoWarn`. Also not covered: the rendered text lines on the canvas (`#text-overlay-container`, js/23:195) — output, not controls. Electron-only rows (`#penWindowMouseReturn`, `#transparentRestartRow`) are described from source; the dump is the web build. I did not run the app; everything here is read from the dump and the source.

Checks on the brief's hints: js/35 (zoom), js/31 (cursor) and js/13 (light source) place **nothing** in Display today. `#zoomModeToggle` (35:111) is not in any of these three sections (no dump record in scope), `#cursorToggle` is moved into the Brush panel (20:5923) and `#lightSourceControls` into Effects (20:4465). Display's contributors are js/05e, 05h, 02, 08, 36, 47 and index.html.

### Conventions used in the tables

- **Selector**: an id where one exists; otherwise a stable path from the section root. `SEC(n)` abbreviates `#sidebar-right > :nth-child(n of .sidebar-section)` with n = **10 Text, 11 Focus, 12 Display** (the 1-based right-bar section index; corrected after the critique — the slice wrote `nth-of-type(12 / 13 / 14)`, the dump's `domOrder`, which resolve to Focus / Display / Export because `.rsb-rail` is the bar's first child, js/20:2584, so `nth-of-type` would need 11 / 12 / 13 at HEAD and one more in a compact window, js/13-compact-mode.js:56–62; a user profile hiding sections via js/43 keeps the DOM order, `.ui-hidden` only hides). Rows marked "(no id)" need this path.
- **Element / role**: the dump's computed role (`cdp`) or page-side fallback (`fallback`, used where Chrome built no AX node, i.e. everything hidden in a `display:none` sub-block at dump time).
- **Sliders**: every `input[type=range]` in a `.control-group` is wrapped in a `.fader-stack` by js/06-slider-updater.js (`ensureStack` 67–95, via a MutationObserver 138–160) and gets `--min/--max/--val` written on `input`/`change` (`onInput` 19–23) — the heat fill in css/slider-styles.css reads them. Text's own `slider()` also writes them when set from code (20:6421–6437). This is the common slider state path and is not repeated per row.
- **Key caps**: js/17-hotkey-reminder.js appends a `span.hk-cap` (17:144–153) to the row of a bound control and an empty `span.hk-cap.hk-cap-empty` to every other `.control-group > label` in both sidebars (17:157–171; on checkbox rows the slot goes on the row itself). Not in the dump (not interactive). Caps in scope: F on the Focus Mode row (17:119), F11 in the Window Mode label (17:120), H and L on the two canvas-border rows (17:117–118).
- **Persistence vocabulary**: *registry* = `js/01a-param-registry.js` SLIDERS/CHECKBOXES/SELECTS → saved as `slider.<id>` / `checkbox.<id>` / `select.<id>` by js/11-settings-interface.js (69–167) **only when Save settings runs** (js/12-save-load.js:196–200) and restored by `applyFromSettings` (12:340–365; `setCheck` 12:64 dispatches `change`, `applySliderValue` 12:52–63 dispatches `input`). *settingsManager* = a bespoke key written directly through `window.settingsManager.set` (js/09). *config* = `window.config.*`. *none* = DOM/JS state only.
- **Sync vocabulary**: *shared* = part of the room look (js/06b): outgoing as a look delta, incoming through `applyLookSections` (06b:258–266 → `applyPresetSnapshot` 12:1033 under `window.__mpApplyingRemote`) or, for sliders in the same units, `mpGlideTo` (06b:573 → js/06c `glideWrite` 101–110, which dispatches `input` and, on landing, `change`). *personal* = stripped by `dropPersonal` (06b:79–95) through `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS` (06b:40–45) or `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL` (06b:56–66). *shared (content)* = Text only: the **line** travels (`text-line` messages, js/06d:1909–1936), not the control. *n/a* = a command or a thing with no setting.

### Text (SEC(10), `data-group="expressive"`, collapsed by default)

Everything in this section edits **the selected line** (`textOverlays.getSelectedId()`, js/23:835–840 `select`) through `commit(props)` (20:6458–6468 → `textOverlays.update` 23:374–389 → `save()` 23:445–456 → settingsManager **`text.overlays`**, write-through on every mutation, 23:63) and re-fills itself from `onChange` (20:7170–7177 → `syncEditor` 7120–7154, suppressed for our own edits by `suppressSync` 20:6334/6465). Remote: a partner's edit to our line arrives as `text-line` with `own` (06d:2037–2049 → `applyRoomEdit` 23:2477–2497 → `updateOverlay` → `emitChange` → the same `syncEditor`). Line fields that ride the wire are `LOOK_KEYS` (23:2132–2134: content, textCase, fontFamily, fontSize, fontWeight, fontStyle, letterSpacing, lineHeight, align, color, opacity, shadow, bgEnabled, bgColor, bgOpacity, padding, radius, rotation) plus position and collider mode/strength (`wireLines` 23:2157–2170). The hotkey fields are **not** on the wire (a key is this user's binding; 20:6940–6945 hides the row for room lines).

Label records in the dump for this section (so the critic's diff finds them): [509] "Letter Spacing", [511] "Line Height", [513] "Opacity", [518] "Background Opacity", [527] "Wall Strength" are `label[for]` elements built by `slider()` and carry the `.value-display` span; [522] "Drop shadow", [524] "Fluid collides with this text", [532] "Pour at the brush location" are the wrapping labels from `checkboxRow()`. The dump's `visibleNow:false` for everything from [499] to [537] and [539]–[541] is the editor being `display:none` with no line selected (20:6612) and the ask being closed, not a hidden feature.

Section-wide notes for Text: (1) almost every element is styled by an inline `style.cssText` string, not a class — `FIELD` 20:6336–6337, `rowEl` 6339–6343, `field` captions 6349–6353, buttons 6475/6480/6511/6724/6758/6960/6981/6990/7010/7034/7052/7058, list rows 6533–6540/6577–6591, ask box 7043–7046; the only classes are `btn--emphasis`, `btn--ghost`, `btn--destructive`, `text-swatch`, `control-group`, `checkbox-group`, `value-display`, `hk-picker*`, `hk-cap`, `mp-owner-dot`. (2) The pink accent `rgba(255,130,170,…)` for the selected row (20:6534–6535, 6578–6579) is also the arrange chrome's colour (23:509). (3) Keyboard: the list rows have no role/tabIndex, so a line can only be chosen by pointer; every other control is a native element.

### Display (SEC(12), `data-group="system"`, collapsed by default)

## A8 — Right sidebar 6: Export, Settings, quality underbar `#quality-underbar`

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A8-right-6.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

Slice of workstream A (`prep/briefs/A-inventory.md`). Enumeration of record: `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json` (generated 2026-10-17T07:41:00.663Z, HEAD `c4796da`, Chrome 155 headless, 1584×805 CSS px, `--ui-scale` 1, scratch profile = app defaults, every section opened). Records for this slice: `surface: sidebar-right, section: Export` (24), `section: Settings` (92), `surface: overlay:quality-underbar` (10) = 126 records. Lazy UI not in the dump is listed from the builders and marked **(lazy, not in dump)** or **(Electron only, not in dump)**.

**Counts.** Interactive controls in the dump: **81** (Export 14, Settings 57, underbar 10); the other 45 records are 43 `<label>` rows and the 2 `.sidebar-section` containers. Listed from builders in addition: Export Output Folder (2, Electron), Settings ComfyUI block (4, Electron), Saved-preset rows (3 per preset, lazy), hotkey list rows (3 per binding, lazy), underbar option rows (25 `role=option`, built on open), slowdown cell (2), demo clock (1, demo edition), Reset app hint (1, first run). Sections: **2** (Export, Settings) + the quality underbar chrome surface.

**Build order for this slice.** `buildSidebar` (js/20-mixer-layout.js:2611) appends `buildExportSection()` (8022) then `buildSettingsSection(controls)` (8390) as the last two right-bar sections, stamps `--rows`/`--i` per `data-group` (`stampGroupRows` 2657-2668), then `buildQualityUnderbar(sidebar)` (2675) appends `#quality-underbar` to `<body>` (2719). After js/20, four scripts mount into Settings by finding the section **by title text** (`findSection('Settings')`): js/43-ui-visibility.js:361 inserts `.ui-vis-block` first (`body.insertBefore(block, body.firstChild)` 43:406), js/49-hotkey-binds.js:1373 inserts `.hk-block` after it (49:1431-1433), js/58-radial-menu.js:861 inserts `.hk-block.radial-block` after that (58:928-930). Scripts load in that order (index.html:1905, 1922, 1927). Four more scripts add to the underbar: js/62-canvas-readout.js:68 (`.qub-size`), js/08a-quality-governor.js:519 (`#slowdownCell`, on demand), js/60-reset-app.js:331 (`#resetAppBtn`), js/52-demo-clock.js:148/469 (`#demoClock` in the demo edition, `#playtestLabel` on the web and itch builds).

**Not covered.** The `.rsb-rail` fold buttons at the top of `#sidebar-right` (dump orders 209-212, section `null`): right-bar shell, another slice. `#statsToggle` "Debug Info": the dump has it in **Display** (order 582); see Open questions 1. The body-mounted dialogs these controls open (`#resetAppModal`, `#hkBindBar`, `#uiForkModal`, `#demoWishlistModal`, `window.confirm`/`appConfirm`): surface 4 ("everything else"); only the opener is listed here. js/50-look-links.js is in this slice's reading list but has no Export UI; its `.look-link-copy` button sits in the Presets panel (50:1098-1109), top-bar slice. js/48-hotkeys.js builds no Settings DOM of its own; its `picker()` (48:577) is what 49 and 58 embed, documented under those rows.

Column key: **State attrs** lists what script toggles (aria-*, classes, `hidden`, `disabled`, inline `style`); **Setting key** names the persisted key (`settingsManager` namespace `fluidUI:`, js/09-settings-manager.js:359) or `config.*`; **Syncs?** is the room look (js/06b-mp-look.js); **State-change path** is the code that already runs when the control changes, for CSS prep step 5.

## A9 — Everything else: titlebar, canvas overlays, hotkey sheet, radial menu, popovers, modals, toasts, pen launcher

Source: `prep/work/inventory/A9-elsewhere.md` (slice report; headings demoted one level, open questions moved to the end of this file, duplicate rows merged as noted inline).

Slice of workstream A (brief `prep/briefs/A-inventory.md`). Written 2026-10-17 against HEAD `c4796da`.

### Header

**Scope.** Every UI surface that is not `#mixer-strip`, `#sidebar-left` or `#sidebar-right`: the boot splash, the PhotoSafe dialog, the startup fork, the Electron title bar and window-resize handles, the skip menu, the two sidebar fold tabs in `#main-area`, the overlays over `#canvas-area` (resize handles and size readout, the Debug Info panel, the brush cursor and ghost, kaleido / mandala / stroke-lock guides, zoom badge, text-arrange chrome, the layer-transform overlay, the recording take prompt and countdown, the hover-capture box, the eyedropper veil and loupe, the focus-mode hint, the floating slowdown cell), the F1 hotkey sheet and the in-place key caps, the radial menu, the popovers (colour picker, arm-tip menu, preset `⋯` menu, the Swirl Together Invite and `⋯` popovers, the palette-from-image panel), the modals (`.delete-modal` family: delete palette, reset app, app confirm / alert, the close ask, the startup fork, Open in Desktop, the demo wishlist ask; the SAM and depth download modals; the mask editor), the toasts and live regions, file drop and paste, the pen window launcher, and the Audio studio drawer.

**Enumeration of record.** `prep/baseline/dom/controls.json` (845 records, captured 2026-10-17T07:41Z, Chrome 155 headless, 1584×805 CSS px, DPR 1, `--ui-scale` 1, web build) for the surfaces `titlebar` (3), `main-area` (2), `canvas-area` (9), `overlay:skipMenu` (4), `overlay:photoWarn` (7), `overlay:hotkeyOverlay` (5), `overlay:deletePaletteModal` (2), `overlay:focus-mode-hint` (1), `overlay:mpInvitePop` (9), `overlay:mpRoomMenu` (3), `overlay:recDrawer` (55) and `overlay:.controls` (41, orphans, see Open questions); plus the four lazy dumps `lazy-radial-menu.json` (10), `lazy-colour-picker.json` (18), `lazy-arm-tip-menu.json` (7) and, for the preset `⋯` menu's anchor only, `lazy-presets-popup.json`. Everything else below is created lazily and was taken from its builder, marked **(lazy, not in dump)**. Dump `o` numbers are quoted in Notes so the critic can join rows to records.

**Counts.** 31 surface tables (a 32nd, the orphaned `.controls` panel, was added after the critique: 15 rows, not counted in the 210 below); **210 control rows** (numbered rows across all tables, a range like `2-6` counting as its span; chrome rows are lettered `c1…` and not counted; the 41 orphans are not counted). Per table: 1 Splash 0, 2 PhotoSafe 5, 3 Startup fork 2, 4 Title bar 11, 5 Skip menu 4, 6 Fold tabs 2, 7 Canvas chrome 11, 8 Debug Info 5, 9 Brush cursor 0, 10 Guides 0, 11 Zoom view 0 (badge counted in 7), 12 Text arrange 1, 13 Layer transform 2, 14 Recording / export 2, 15 Hover capture 9, 16 Eyedropper 0, 17 Hotkey sheet 5, 18 Key caps 0, 19 Radial menu 10, 20 Colour picker 23, 21 Arm-tip menu 6, 22 Preset menu 3, 23 Swirl Together popovers 10, 24 Palette from image 8, 25 Modals 18 (delete palette 2, reset app 4, app confirm 2, close ask 3, Open in Desktop 4, demo wishlist 3), 26 SAM / depth download 0, 27 Mask editor 48, 28 Toasts 0, 29 File drop 0, 30 Pen launcher 1, 31 Audio drawer 24.

**Not covered.** `#mixer-more-panel` and the Presets panel itself (top-bar slice; only the per-preset `⋯` menu is here); the quality underbar and everything mounted in it (`#resetAppBtn`, the normal `#slowdownCell`, `.qub-size` from js/62, `#demoClock`, `#playtestLabel`: right-sidebar slice, which the brief ends with the underbar); the Settings → Interface, Hotkeys and Radial Menu blocks (js/43:359-460, js/49:1377-1550, js/58:861-1045: Settings section slice); `#lightDot` (inside the Effects section's light grid, js/13:37); the pen window's own document (a separate window with its own `<style>`, js/47); `phone/`; the no-WebGL fatal screen (js/04a:13-30, only when the app cannot run); Electron's native fallback dialogs (js/04f:725-738); `window.alert` in js/16:240. Sync for everything here is **n/a** unless a row says otherwise: these surfaces are chrome, or they write a control that lives in another slice (the row then names that control and defers to its slice for the sync column).

**Column key.** Same nine columns as the brief. "State-change path" names the function that runs when the control changes (and, for shared controls, the incoming handler); for chrome it names what shows and hides it. Selectors without an id are given as the dump's stable `parent .class:nth-of-type(n)` form.

---

### 32. Orphaned static panel `overlay:.controls` (added after the critique; dump `o` 700-740, 41 records, 15 interactive, none user-reachable)

The static `div.controls` (index.html:636 onward) is the pre-mixer control panel. At boot js/20 re-parents most of its children into the strip and the sidebars and hides what is left: `controls.style.display = 'none'` (js/20:90) and `body > .controls { display: none !important }` (css/20-mixer-strip.css:221–223). What stays behind is dead on screen but alive to script: every id below still has a `getElementById` binding (js/12 `setupUserPresets` 2239–2245, js/04c:295, js/04d:317–345, 389, 565, 581, js/04e:427) or an inline `onclick`, so a later "prune dead markup" step must check those handlers, and workstreams B (selector contract) and C (unmatched CSS) need the ids. `section` is `null` for all 41 records and `visibleAtBoot` is false; none can be reached by a user. The merge named this group (Totals, X11, A9 open question 3) but did not list it. The shipping preset save UI is the Presets panel (A1 §3.1); the shipping animations are in Animations (A6).

, plus what the merge found. The merge's own items are also in `prep/work/open-items/A-merge.md`; each slice's items are also in `prep/work/open-items/<slice>.md`. Nothing here is decided: behaviour, sync, performance and script selectors are frozen.

### Cross-slice themes (merge summary; the numbered lists per slice follow unchanged)

| # | Theme | Where it was raised | What it means for the reskin |
|---|---|---|---|
| X1 | **`--i` is already taken.** `stampGroupRows` (js/20:2657–2669) and js/43 `restampGroups` (43:280–296) write inline `--rows` and `--i` on every `.sidebar-section[data-group]`; css/21-sidebar.css:183–184 reads `--i` for the title gradient slice. Custom properties inherit, so every control in a section already inherits an integer `--i`. | A4 1, A5 1, A7 1 | CSS prep step 5's per-control `--i` (brightness) must be renamed, or set on every element that reads it, or the section property renamed (a script change). Decide before step 5. |
| X2 | **Section titles are a soft contract.** Collapse state (`sidebar.sections`), js/43's `section:<title>` hide keys and `SIMPLE_KEEP`, js/43/49/58 mounting into Settings by title, `window.Sidebars.find(title)`, the left rail buttons, js/44 tour targets all key on the title text. | A2 11, A3 13, A6 13, A7 12, A8 9 | `text-transform` is fine; changing DOM text in the theme pass orphans saved state and breaks mounts. |
| X3 | **Toggle state as `.active` only, no ARIA state.** Limit (inverted: `.active` = off), Ignite, Mutate Scope Basic/All, Simple/Everything, Italic, Align ×3, Arrange on Canvas, Focus formats ×4, `#penWindowBtn`, Saved-preset rows, running Quick Export buttons, pattern tiles, `.studio-tab`, mask tool tabs. | A1 1–2, A5 2, A7 3, A8 4, A9 5, 9 | Step 4 wants `[aria-pressed]` rules beside class rules; these have nothing to bind to without adding attributes (a markup change that is arguably "accessibility up"). Decide once for the `.ch-seg-switch` pattern. |
| X4 | **Inline `style.cssText` / `style=""` carries much of the styling** in Text (almost every element), Export, Settings (preset and ComfyUI blocks), the two light panels in Effects, the left bar's ramp rows / alt-size rows / selects, the canvas overlays (`#brushCursor`, guides, `#zoomBadge`), the SAM / depth modals, five toasts, `#photoWarn`'s own `<style>`. | A2 5, A3 4, A5 5, A7 5, A8 5, A9 6 | Step 2's tokens cannot reach values inside `style=""`; css/21-sidebar.css:932–949 already fights two of them with `!important`. Decide whether moving inline values to classes (markup-only, behaviour-neutral) is in scope. |
| X5 | **Visibility and "disabled" by inline `style.display` / `style.opacity`**, not class or attribute: `#mpDisconnected` / `#mpConnected`, `#multigridPanel`, the collider source menu, `#previewToggle`, `#lightSourceControls` / `#lightShiftControls`, `#audioMini` / `#audioSceneBox` / `#audioPlayRow`, `#mandalaPanel`, `.anim-lib-list`, `#recKeepRow`, Text's `syncBgEnabled` dim, `setGroupEnabled` greys. | A2 4, A3 5, A4 6, A6 2, A7 4 | A theme can select present / absent, not these states, without `[style*=…]`; adding `hidden` / a class beside them is a script change. |
| X6 | **Unnamed or title-named controls** (AX name "" or a long `title`): `#mutationCount`, `#lightMode`, the light / light-shift sliders, `#kSpinSpeed`, `#audioReactSource`, the drawer's Lead / Nudge / Similarity, `canvas.ace-canvas[aria-label=""]`, the three alternate-size sliders, `#replayTimePeriod`, `#splatIn/OutMode` and `Dist`, `#textOverlayFont` and four Text selects, the Export duration / fps fields, `.cp-num`, `#statsCloseBtn`, `#studioCloseBtn`, the titlebar buttons, the Interface "Settingsalways shown" row, the file inputs. | A1 6–7, A2 2–3, A4 7, A5 4, A6 1, A7 6–7, A8 3, 10, A9 10 | Baseline facts for F3 ("accessibility only goes up"); fixing them is a markup change for Gabriel, not prep. |
| X7 | **Pointer-only controls / no keyboard path**: preset group heads, `#curlValue` shape cycling, the gravity / light / light-shift pads, `.mutation-card` and chain crumbs, `.anim-lib-row`, `.anim-slot-clear`, the text list rows, the canvas and capture-area handles, `.pfi-marker`, `.cp-swatch-old`, `#zoomBadge`, the layer-row collapse caret. | A1 8, A3 10, A4 7, A5 3, A6 8, A7 2, A9 11 | Same as X6: record, do not fix in prep. |
| X8 | **States the baseline cannot show** without scripting or Electron: parked More panel (<900 px), a room, every non-Sim layer row, Quick Export running, the slowdown button, saved presets / hotkeys, Electron-only rows, the `.pen-screen` radios, Transparent Background's dependent group, the open `.qub-dd` list, every lazy modal / overlay. | A1 13, A4 10, A7 8, A8 6, 8, A9 17 | F1's screenshot set and F3's axe run need an explicit list of which of these they drive and which stay unbaselined. |
| X9 | **Where the theme's runtime properties can ride (step 5).** Local: each control's own `input` / `change` handler (cited per row). Remote: `glideWrite` (06c:101–118) and `applyPresetSnapshot` (12:1033) re-enter the same handlers under `window.__mpApplyingRemote`; the sender is known only in `window.__mpRoomLookChanged(change, fromId)` (06b:598 → 06e:950 `onRoomLookChanged`), whose `flashControl` already writes `--mp-peer` on the control's face. Gaps: material / palette / gravity / layers / text changes arrive as **sections** and get no `flashControl`; rebuilt widgets (palette carousel, layer rows, anim slots, lane rows) lose any property set on the old nodes. | A1 5 (table), 9, A3 8, A4 4–5, A5 summary table, A6 4, A7 10 | Decide which element carries `--ga` / `--gc` / `--pc` (face vs input) and whether `--peer` / `--mp-peer` / `--pc` unify; set properties inside the render functions for rebuilt widgets. |
| X10 | **Registry / `params.json` drift** (data, not UI): stale defaults (`curl`, `viscosity`, `velocityDissipation`, `wetInfluence`, `wetDrying`), stale line numbers (Audio), `shadeFormResolution` missing, option counts for the resolution selects, `configKey: null` on `glowIntensity` / `glowThreshold` / `scatterReach` deciding glide vs step, `audioAutoSplatMode` select with no element. | A1 15, A3 12, A5 13, A6 7, A8 2 | For whoever maintains `scripts/test/inventory/params.json`; the registry is the source of truth. |
| X11 | **Dead markup / dead code still in the tree**: the orphaned static `.controls` panel (41 records, ids still read by js/12 / 04c / 04d / 04e), `data-accent` on fader cells (nothing styles it), `.arm-sym-note`, `#paletteStepIndicator` reference, `moveCheckboxGroup('stepPalette')` and `moveCheckboxGroup('statsToggle')` no-ops, `#multiplayerToggle`, `#customCursor` vs `#brushCursor`, two layer-handle systems, five dead `zoom: var(--ui-scale)` selectors (`#colorBar`, `#settingsToggleBtn`, `#settingsPanel`, `.fps-notification`, `#recipePill`). | A1 10, A2 6, A3 3, A4 2, 8, A8 1, A9 1–3, F0 | Prune candidates for Gabriel; prep leaves them. C's unmatched-rule audit should confirm the CSS ones. |
