### Critique pass (A-critic → this revision, 2026-10-17)

`prep/work/inventory-critique.md` checked this file against the dump and 38 sampled rows. Everything it found was applied in place (see the Revision line in the header); its three open questions are in `prep/work/open-items/A-critic.md` and are repeated here because they decide how the next workstreams use this inventory:

| # | Item |
|---|---|
| K1 | **Section selectors.** The ten positional selectors in A3–A7 pointed at the wrong section (`.rsb-rail` is `#sidebar-right`'s first child, js/20:2584). They now read `#sidebar-right > :nth-child(k of .sidebar-section)` (Sections table, "Position selector" column). Should E and the theme key on that form, or should CSS prep step 4 add a per-section attribute no script reads (`data-ui="drawer"` + e.g. `data-ui-section="effects"`) so nothing keys on position? Gabriel's call. |
| K2 | **Orphaned `.controls` ids** (`ss*`, `userPreset*`, `saveAsPresetBtn`, `shootingStarToggle`, `jellyfishBtn`, `portalBtn`, now listed in A9 §32) keep live `getElementById` bindings while `display:none`. Keep them on B's do-not-rename list, or are they slated for removal? Report only. Also from §32: the hidden Origin picker's `ssOrigin` is still part of the preset snapshot and the look allow-list (js/12:775–791, 1019; js/06b:175, 203, 225). |
| K3 | **Interface checkboxes** (Settings, 28 rows) are addressable only through the `data-ui-key` script contract (js/43:442). Is a parallel styling hook wanted, or is `label.ui-vis-row` + `:checked` / `:disabled` / `.is-pinned` enough for the theme? |
| K4 | **`—` cells.** 487 cells stay `—` under the legend added to "How to read the tables" rather than being rewritten to `none` / `n/a` (which `_merge.js` could do as a post-pass). Say if the explicit words are wanted. |

### A1 — Top bar #mixer-strip

1. **Limit's pressed state is inverted and has no ARIA state.** `.active` on `.ch-gate-toggle` means the limit is **off** (js/20:1330–1336) while on every other bar button `.active` means "on"; the On/Off word is CSS-generated (css/20:481–490) and the only machine-readable state is the hidden `#colorGate.checked` (`aria-hidden`). CSS-prep step 4 cannot style it through `[aria-pressed]` without adding the attribute, and adding `aria-pressed` changes the control's announced role/state (a11y change, arguably "up"). Decide: add `aria-pressed` (value = limit off, matching `.active`) in prep, or leave the class-only hook.
2. **Ignite has no state attribute and is not on the wire.** State is `.active`/`.locked` on the button and `.holding`/`.armed` on the row (js/20:1386–1399); the lock does carry `aria-pressed` (1498–1500). The README's decided item ("holding is shared and shows the holder's glow") needs a new multiplayer message: js/06a–06e carry no nudge/ignite event, and `DyeNudge.level()` is never written to the DOM (05h:131–218). Both are behaviour/sync changes, out of prep scope; the theme can only light the holder's own button today.
3. **Clear has no id.** js/20:1526 finds it by `button[onclick*="clearCanvas"]`; the only class is `.btn--destructive`. A stable styling hook (`data-ui`) should be added in prep; the inline `onclick` and `.btn--destructive` go on the selector contract.
4. **`type=submit` buttons.** `#mixerPresetsTrigger` (js/20:1739, no `type`), Clear, `#pauseBtn`, `#freezeBtn` and the 12 built-in preset buttons (index.html:1359–1370, 1482–1484) are `type=submit` (dump). Harmless with no `<form>`, but `button[type=button]` selectors in a theme would miss them. Report only.
5. **Material slider labelling.** The planning doc's decided labels are Vorticity / Wetness / Thickness; the code's macro names are `'Flow'` (29:53) and `'Thickness'` (29:79), surfaced only through the slider's `title` (29:149, 223). No visible label changes with the material, and the slider's accessible name stays "Fluid" (nameFader, js/20:1195) in every mode. A label that follows the material is a markup/behaviour addition; "Wetness" vs "Flow" is a naming decision for Gabriel.
6. **The `#curlValue` readout is a clickable `span` in Paint (Thick) mode** (29:320–326, cursor/title 29:134–140) with no role and no tab stop: the stamp shape can only be changed with a pointer. A11y item to report, not a prep change.
7. **Orphan labels still name strip inputs.** The static `.controls` panel (`display:none`, 41 records in `overlay:.controls`) keeps `label[for]` elements that the AX tree uses when an input has no `aria-label`: the hidden canonicals read "⏱️ Time" (index.html:919), "Density Sustain" (:731), "Laminar / Blend" (:800); the visible ones carry an `aria-label` and so also have two names (`labelFor` "Motion Isolation", "Velocity Sustain", "Fluid Color", "Random Colors", "Palette mode (new colour each stroke)"). Ties to F0's open question on pruning `.controls`; any pruning is a markup change with a11y effect.
8. **Preset group heads are not keyboard reachable.** `.preset-group-head` is a `div` with a `click` handler (js/20:1831–1842), no role, no `tabindex`; collapse/expand (persisted in `presets.groups.collapsed`) is pointer-only. The ⋯ on named groups is a real button. Report for the a11y baseline (F3).
9. **A peer's material change does not flash the Fluid cell.** `onRoomLookChanged` flashes slider/checkbox/select keys only (06e:391–394); `material` is a section and only reaches `#mpActivity` text. If the theme's `--ga`/`--pc` ride on `flashControl`, the Material Type cell gets no remote glow without a small change to 06e. Report only.
10. **`data-accent` is dead.** Set on every fader cell (js/20:1130, 1206) but nothing styles it (css/20:268–271). Candidate theme hook or candidate for removal: Gabriel's call; keep it as is during prep.
11. **Current preset is a JS variable.** `currentUserPreset` (js/20:1752) drives the trigger label text, `.has-preset` on the label span and `.active` on list rows; there is no attribute on the trigger that says "a preset is loaded". Styling a "preset loaded" state needs `.has-preset` (a class on a child) or a new attribute.
12. **Brush Size in js/43 and js/46.** The strip is attached to the DOM (js/20:65) before `buildLeftSidebar` moves the Brush Size cell out (:77, 2391–2394); js/46 builds from a body `MutationObserver` (46:347–349) and js/43 collects later, so neither should ever see `strip:Brush Size` as a strip item, although 43:117 still lists it in a comment and the cell keeps `data-ui-key="Brush Size"`. Not verified live; A2 or the critic should confirm with the headless driver.
13. **More panel contents not dumped.** `#mixer-more-panel` only fills below ~900 CSS px at scale 1; the dump (1584 px) saw it hidden. Its rows are the live fader cells (same records), so nothing is missing from the enumeration, but the parked rendering (css/20:1590–1603) was not captured. F1 should add a narrow-width screenshot.
14. **Ownership overlap with A4.** The Presets panel, its ⋯ menu and `#presetPathToast` are created by `buildPresetsChannel` (my builder) and are also "popovers … preset menu" in the brief's surface 4; the colour picker `.cp-pop` is referenced here but tabled by A4. The merge agent should dedupe against A4's tables.
15. **Stale `scripts/test/inventory/params.json` defaults.** `curl` def 25 vs registry 0 (01a:56), `viscosity` def 0 vs 0.3 (01a:75), `velocityDissipation` def 0.999 vs 1.0009 (01a:29); the binding line for the generic sim sliders is 05h:550–619 (params.json says 569). The registry is the source of truth; report for whoever maintains params.json.
16. **Silent writes bypass the state-change path.** Material modes write `#velocityDissipation.value`/`--val`/readout with no event (29:113–123), and Ctrl+scroll writes `brushSize`/`curl`/`velocityInfluence`/`velocityDissipation`/`densityDissipation` and then dispatches `input` (05h:360–465, 507–527). Any runtime property set from the `input` handler misses the material's silent write (the 250 ms proxy poll only covers curved faders, js/20:1114–1119). Report; a change would be behaviour.

### A2 — Left sidebar #sidebar-left (head, Brush, Multi-Brush, Stroke and replay, rail)

Also written to `prep/work/open-items/A2-left-sidebar.md`.

1. **Hold symmetry is shared; the rest of the bar is personal.** `symmetryHold` is registry category `simulation` (js/01a-param-registry.js:80), so `isPersonalSlider` (js/06b-mp-look.js:70-75) lets it through and the glide applies peers' values (js/06c-mp-glide.js:101,156). The README's "the left brush sidebar stays personal" is therefore not quite the whole truth; the theme's remote-glow properties (`--ga`, `--gc`, `--pc`) would apply to exactly one control in this bar. Confirm this is intended (behaviour is frozen either way; this only affects which hooks the theme adds).
2. **Three range inputs with no id and no accessible name** (alternate-brush size, one per mouse button; js/20:4847-4856, dump orders 136/152/168, `name: ""`). The only stable selector is positional. Styling them per state needs either an id/aria-label (markup, but "accessibility only goes up" would welcome a name) or a selector through `#altSizeOn_<side>`'s parent. Gabriel to decide whether adding `aria-label` in prep is in scope.
3. **Controls whose AX name is empty**: `#replayTimePeriod` (its heading label has no `for`, js/20:5064-5066), `#splatInMode` / `#splatOutMode` (no label element, 5146-5156 / 5197-5204), `#splatInDist` / `#splatOutDist` (inline `span` captions, 5164-5185), and the file input (6030-6034). F3's a11y baseline will record these as-is; fixing them is a markup change, so it is a decision for Gabriel, not prep.
4. **Greyed states are inline `style.opacity`, not a class or attribute**: `setGroupEnabled` (js/20:6110-6115; Strength, Texture via `syncTexState` 6070), the alternate size slider (4998), the per-arm Pressure switch (8946), the arm tip square (675) and the arm colour well (8985). The theme cannot restyle "inert" through CSS alone; `:disabled` is also set for the slider groups (6114, 6071), which the theme can use, but the buttons have only the inline opacity. Report for E (mapping) and the CSS plan.
5. **Inline `style.cssText` on structural elements** the theme would want to restyle: the paint-lock warning (4631-4633), both ramp rows and their captions (5165-5178), the alternate size row and readout (4848, 4854), the alt summary (4891), the alt shape note (4823), each button box (4746), the three `SECTION_SELECT_CSS` selects (4757) and the two splat selects (5147, 5198), the symmetry note (8657). Inline styles beat every cascade layer; listing them is C's job, the decision (move to classes = markup-only change, behaviour-neutral) is Gabriel's.
6. **Dead elements still in layout**: `.arm-sym-note` always has empty text (js/20:8694) but keeps `padding: 2px 0 6px` (8657), and `.button-roles-lock`'s message ends in a dead `+ ''` (4638-4639). Pruning changes nothing visible except 8 px; flagged, not decided.
7. **Dump oddity**: `#replaySpeed` shows `value: "1.25"` while its readout says "1×" and the built default is `'1'` (js/20:5103, dump order 185). Either the scratch profile carried a value with the readout not refreshed at build, or the dump's trusted Shift-clicks nudged the slider. F0/F1 should re-check before using it as a pixel baseline.
8. **Per-frame loop while Multi-Brush is open**: `watchTick` (js/20:9128-9133) runs on every animation frame and compares `panelSig()` (9084-9107, several `getElementById`s and string joins) but writes DOM only on a change. It is today's baseline, so performance is frozen with it; CSS prep step 5 should hook `paintRow` (8935), never the tick.
9. **The Brush Size channel keeps `data-ui-key="Brush Size"`** and js/43 lists `strip:` keys by walking `[data-ui-key]` (43:134-138). Whether it can actually be hidden from the Interface list (the comment at 43:55-56 says it "is always on") was not verified in the live app.
10. **`#multiplierValue[data-mirror]`** is written by script (js/20:8711) and drawn by CSS; B should carry it on the selector contract as a write hook.
11. **Section titles are data**: `sidebar.sections` persists by title text (js/20:9201-9210), `Sidebars.find` matches on it (2506-2513) and the rail buttons copy it (2452). Any rename in the reskin is a behaviour change (lost collapse state).
12. **Deferred until the study mapping (E):** which study component each row maps to (`dial`, `telegraph`, `pane`, `field`, `keycap`, `tube`, `lamp`, `drawer`). The study's left cabinet is Brush 653 / Multi-brush 726 / Input 763 (v19); this bar's third section is "Stroke and replay", which has no obvious study counterpart, and the Stabilization/Hide guide pair sits in the head here, not in a section.

### A3 — Right sidebar 1: Swirl Together, Simulation

Written to `prep/work/open-items/A3-right-1.md` as well.

1. **Scope vs DOM.** The slice named the gravity pad and the resolution selects under Simulation; in the shipping DOM they are in Effects and the quality underbar. Inventoried as appendix cross-references only. The merge agent should dedupe against the Effects and underbar slices and keep one row set.
2. **FPS Limit appears to be shared over the room.** `fpsCap` is in the registry's `SELECTS` (js/01a:280) so `capturePresetSnapshot` carries it (js/12:133, :169–177), and neither `ROOM_LOOK_PERSONAL.selects` (js/06b:60) nor `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS` (:39–45) drops it, while look links (js/50:82) and the defaults baseline (js/12:1850) both exclude it as per-machine. The receiver only applies it when its own, Hz-dependent option list holds the value (js/12:1124–1125), so behaviour differs per screen. Intended? Any fix is a sync change, outside prep.
3. **`#multiplayerToggle`** (index.html:1389) is an unlabelled, `display:none` checkbox kept "for legacy compat" (js/20:8371), read by `initMultiplayer` (js/06a:399–406). AX name is empty. Prune candidate; until then the theme must keep it `display:none`.
4. **Inline layout styles** in the panel: `#reconnectBtn` carries `width:100%; margin-top:8px` inline (index.html:1409); the two Simulation checkbox labels carry `style="margin: 0"` (index.html:752, :756). CSS prep step 2 cannot tokenise these without touching markup; list for C/E.
5. **State held as inline `style.display`, not class/attribute**: `#mpDisconnected` / `#mpConnected` (view), `#roomDisplay`, `#shareHint`, `#copyRoomRow`, `#lockRoomBtn`, `#mpMenuBtn`, `#mpActivity`, `#mpError`, `#reconnectBtn`, `#roomQr`, `#roomName` (QR mode), `#multigridPanel` (gate), `#pressureFieldControls`. Styling "present/absent" is fine; styling a *dimmed-but-present* gated panel (e.g. multigrid sliders greyed instead of hidden) would need a behaviour change.
6. **Room-level state with no DOM hook**: "≥2 people here" exists only as `.mp-dot-connected` on `#connectionDot` and `.mp-activity.live`; "host vs guest" only as `#lockRoomBtn`'s display; "stranger vs private" nowhere. If the theme wants to tint the drawer by room state, the natural place to add a `data-*` is `updateConnectedView` (js/06e:524–581) — prep must not do it.
7. **Open-popover styling**: `#mpInviteBtn` / `#mpMenuBtn` show open state as `aria-expanded="true"` + `.active`; css/01-buttons.css styles `.active` and `[aria-pressed="true"]` (:148–155) but not `[aria-expanded="true"]`. Prep step 4 should add the ARIA rule beside `.active` (no behaviour change).
8. **Runtime properties for step 5**: `flashControl` (js/06e:373–384) already writes `--mp-peer` and `.mp-peer-touch` on the changed control's *face* (`.control-group` / `.checkbox-group` / `.mixer-channel`), and the cursor tag writes `--peer` (:171); `cursorColour(id)` (:146–149) is the per-peer colour. The theme's `--ga` / `--gc` / `--pc` can be set in that same function. Decision for Gabriel/E: which element carries them (face vs input), and whether `--peer` / `--mp-peer` / `--pc` are unified.
9. **Governor-capped values are JS-only.** Pressure Iteration and V-Cycles show the request; `QualityGovernor` may lower the effective count (params.json `gatedBy`, `mapping`). No DOM attribute says so; a "governed" look would need a behaviour change.
10. **Gravity pad has no keyboard path**: `.pressure-pad` is a plain div with pointer handlers (js/20:4263–4294), no role or tabindex, not in the dump. "Accessibility only goes up" — flag for the Effects owner and F3.
11. **FPS Limit options vary by refresh rate** across headless runs (F0 pinned it out of `--check`); any screenshot or axe baseline that includes Simulation must pin or exclude that row.
12. **params.json defaults are stale** for `wetInfluence` (0 vs registry 0.14, index.html 0.14) and `wetDrying` (3 vs 4.5). Inventory data only; not a UI issue.
13. **Section title text is a soft contract**: `sidebar.sections` and `ui.hiddenSections` keys, js/43's `SIMPLE_KEEP`, and js/44's tour targets all key on "Swirl Together" / "Simulation". A renamed heading in the theme orphans saved state.
14. **`#roomReportBtn`** is appended by js/61 on a 500 ms poll and `#phonePadBtn` / `#phonePadRoomBtn` by js/54 at init; they are in the dump, but a dump taken earlier in boot could miss them. The critic diff should treat these three as lazily present.

### A4 — Right sidebar 2: Colors and palettes, Layers

Also written to `prep/work/open-items/A4-right-2.md`.

1. **`--i` is already taken on `.sidebar-section`.** `stampGroupRows` writes inline `--rows` and `--i` on every right-sidebar section (js/20:2659–2671) and `.section-title` reads `var(--i)` for its gradient slice (css/21-sidebar.css:183–184). The theme's per-control `--i` (brightness, CSS prep step 5) would be inherited by every control inside a section and clash with this value on the section node itself. Needs a rename on one side (report only; both are script-written).
2. **`moveCheckboxGroup('stepPalette', body)` (js/20:4528) is dead.** `#stepPalette` is adopted by the strip's colour channel first (js/20:1282) inside a not-yet-attached subtree, so the Colors section never receives Palette mode; the dump shows it in `mixer-strip | Color` with `aria-hidden="true"` (order 35). Prune the call, or move Palette mode here? Behaviour question for Gabriel.
3. **`[data-cs]` does not exist.** The task text names `[data-cs]` collision controls; the real hooks are `data-collision`, `data-collision-layer`, `data-cinv`, `data-cref` (05k:409, 446, 456, 470). The selector contract (B) should carry those four.
4. **A remote palette change has no per-control glow.** `onRoomLookChanged` only flashes registry ids; `paletteIndex` / `paletteName` / `savedColors` are look sections and reach `#mpActivity` as a sentence (06e:332–336, 390–392). Styling `#paletteSelect` / chips with `--ga`/`--gc`/`--pc` needs a hook in `applyRoomLookChange` or `onRoomLookChanged` (new code path, not a style).
5. **A peer's collider row is only knowable from JS.** `layer.__peerOwner` (06d:249, 06d:508–556) has no DOM reflection on `.layer-item`; tinting a peer's wall with their `--pc` needs an attribute written in `renderLayers`.
6. **States hidden in inline `style`:** the collider source menu opens by `style.display = 'flex'` (js/20:3698) with nothing on `#addCollisionLayerBtn`; `#previewToggle` and `#lightShiftControls` likewise. A theme can only hook `[style*=…]` unless script adds a class/`aria-expanded`.
7. **Accessibility baseline items (report for F3; none are style-only fixes):** layer-row faders (`.encapsulated-slider`) and the Sim name field have no accessible name (`.layer-prop-label` is a span); path-row `<label>`s have no `for`; `.pfi-marker` drag handles are pointer-only `div`s with no role or keyboard path; layer rows and path rows have no `aria-expanded` (the caret / "▲▼" glyph is the only indicator); `#deletePaletteModal` has no `role=dialog`; `clearImageLayerMask` uses `window.confirm` (05l:370), which no theme can style; `#paletteSelect` is a `div[role=combobox]` whose body-mounted list is not linked by `aria-controls`.
8. **`#paletteStepIndicator` is referenced but never exists** (01:972; no element in index.html or any builder). Dead code to prune.
9. **Legacy palette CSS likely unmatched** (for C to confirm in the live audit): `.palette-tag*`, `.palette-new`, `.palette-tag-input`, `.palette-step`, `#sidebar-right .palette-tag` (css/styles.css:656–672, css/21:1050–1060), and `#sidebar-right .palette-carousel { max-height:160px; overflow-y:auto }` (css/21:1050) is overridden by the later `:is(#sidebar-right, #sidebar-left) .palette-carousel` (css/23:344–350, same specificity, later file).
10. **Baseline fixture needed for Layers.** Only the Sim row exists in the dump/scratch profile; the pixel gate for this section needs one picture, paint, depth-collider, painted-collider and path row (see "Rows the theme must style…"). The screenshot script will have to seed layers without an app setter that persists (GUIDANCE §2b) — e.g. through the paste path or `window.layers` directly.
11. **Both sections are hidden in the Simple layout** (js/43:48–58, `.ui-hidden`). Baselines must run on the Full layout (the dump did); the mapping (E) should note the Simple case renders neither section.
12. **Static buttons default to `type=submit`:** `#uploadBtn`, `#captureBtn`, `.palette-actions button`, `.delete-modal-actions button`, path-row `.layer-btn`s. Harmless (no form), but B should not "normalise" them during prep.
13. **Warm collider tint.** `.collision-controls` / `.collision-source-menu` use `rgba(255,120,80,…)` (css/22-overlays.css:124–160) — the only warm accent in the right sidebar today; the theme doc should say whether copper absorbs it or it stays a semantic "wall" colour.

### A5 — Right sidebar 3: Mutate shader, Effects

1. **`--i` is already in use on every `.sidebar-section`.** `stampGroupRows` (20:2657–2668) writes inline `--rows` and `--i` (the section's row index within its colour group, 0…n) and css/21-sidebar.css:183–184 reads them to slice the group gradient across the titles. Custom properties inherit, so every control in a section would inherit `--i` = its section's row index. The theme's `--i` (brightness 0–1) must take another name, or be set on every control that uses it, or `stampGroupRows` must be renamed later (a script change, outside prep). Decision needed before CSS prep step 5.
2. **Scope segmented toggle has no ARIA state.** Basic/All are two `button.ch-text-toggle` whose only state is the `.active` class (20:2946–2951); the hidden `#mutationScope` is `aria-hidden`. CSS prep step 4 wants an `[aria-pressed]` rule beside the class, but there is no attribute to key on; adding `aria-pressed` is an accessibility improvement, yet a markup change. Same pattern as every other `.ch-seg-switch` in the app, so decide once.
3. **Variant cards and chain crumbs are not keyboard reachable as such.** `.mutation-card` (click, 20:3223) and `.mutation-crumb` (click, 20:3540) are `div`/`span` with no role or tabindex; a card is picked from the keyboard only through its `.mutation-changes` span (`tabindex=0`, Enter/Space 20:3216–3218), which has no role either. Baseline fact for F3; fixing it is behaviour.
4. **Unnamed controls (AX name "" in the dump):** `#mutationCount`, `#lightMode`, `#lightSpeed`, `#lightIntensity`, `#lightAmbient`, `#lightShiftSpeed`, `#lightShiftThreshold`, `#lightShiftIntensity`, `#lightShiftSaturation` (labels without `for`, index.html:1243, 1255, 1261, 1268, 1301, 1308, 1315, 1322); `#mutationStrength` and `#lightShiftMode` are named only by their `title`; the Scope buttons have no group label. "Accessibility only goes up" means the theme may not lose these names, but giving them names is a markup change. Report for F3 and for Gabriel.
5. **Inline styles in the two light panels.** `#lightSourceControls`, `#lightShiftControls` and nine children carry `style=""` attributes with colours, borders and backgrounds (index.html:1241–1330: panel wrappers, `#lightMode`, `#lightGridContainer`, `.grid-line`, `#lightDot`, caption label, `#lightShiftWheel`, `#lightShiftMode`, `#clearLightShiftPath`); css/21-sidebar.css:932–949 already fights them with `!important`. A theme layer cannot override inline styles without `!important` or moving them into CSS (a markup change, no behaviour). Flag for the CSS plan and the C audit; `#mutationScope` (20:2934) and `#lightSpeedControl` (13:131–133 `display:flex`) are also inline-styled from script.
6. **Existing per-frame DOM writes in this section's modules.** js/13 runs a rAF loop at all times (13:230–263; early return when the lamp is off) and, while Light Source is on, writes `#lightDot` `left/top` and the overlay gradient every ~33 ms (13:200–214) even with no change; js/45 writes `#breathCue` `transform` every frame while Breathing is on (45:270). Neither touches `:root`. The frame-time baseline should be taken with both off and once with Light Source on so the theme is not blamed for them; the theme must not add writes on those paths.
7. **Dither's drawn tile is persisted but not shared.** The 16×16 pattern lives in `settingsManager 'dither.tile'` (68:43, 68:163) and is not part of the look (06b `LOOK_ALLOW` 06b:222–225), so a peer who receives Pattern = "Draw your own" renders their own tile. Not a styling matter; listed because the Pattern select *is* shared.
8. **`#ditherPulse` is personal while the rest of the Dither panel is shared** (registry category `audio`, 01a:163; `isPersonalSlider` 06b:71–75). The theme's shared/personal treatment (remote glow, player tint) must follow the registry category per control, not the panel.
9. **`.fx-thumb` is an image acting as a button.** `span[role=img][tabindex=0]` with click/Enter/Space toggling the checkbox (20:4433–4451). Baseline oddity for F3; the styling hook is fine (`:hover`, `:focus-visible`, `.is-empty`).
10. **Hidden-mirror inconsistency.** `#mutationScope` is hidden by an inline style string (20:2934) while the other hidden native selects use `.qub-native-hidden` (20:2758). Styling-contract note for B; no change in prep.
11. **The Effects section has no id.** Its only stable hooks are the title text and DOM position (`#sidebar-right > :nth-child(6 of .sidebar-section)` (= `.sidebar-section:nth-of-type(7)` at HEAD, `.rsb-rail` first, js/20:2584; corrected after the critique)), both soft contracts. CSS prep step 4 allows adding `data-ui`; decide whether sections get `data-ui="drawer"` plus a stable key, and whether that key may be the title.
12. **Mutate's own controls are neither persisted nor shared** (Scope, Strength, Variants, five locks; 20:3121–3134 read them at click time). Confirm the theme treats them as personal (no `--ga`/`--pc`), and that the "N changes" / diff colours (`rgba(180,100,255,…)`, 22:299–470) are free to move to the copper/teal tokens.
13. **Registry `configKey` mismatches** (not styling, recorded for E/F): `glowIntensity`, `glowThreshold`, `scatterReach` are `configKey: null` in 01a (145, 146, 158) while their 05e handlers write `config.GLOW_INTENSITY`, `config.GLOW_THRESHOLD`, `config.SCATTER_DENSITY`; params.json already notes this. It decides glide-vs-step on the wire (06c:58–78), so the sync baseline will show these three stepping.

### A6 — Right sidebar 4: Animations, Audio, Kaleidoscope (+ the Audio drawer #recDrawer)

Written also to `prep/work/open-items/A6-right-4.md`.

1. **Accessible names missing (a11y only goes up, but fixing means markup).** `#kSpinSpeed` has a `<label>` without `for` (index.html:1008–1011; AX name ""). `#audioReactSource` has no label at all (js/20:7244; AX name ""). The drawer's Lead and Nudge sliders have labels without `for` (js/40:1528–1535; names ""). `canvas.ace-canvas` is focusable with `aria-label=""` (js/40b:740–745). The Save/Load cue buttons have empty text and are named by `title` alone (js/40:1699–1706). Report for Gabriel: adding `for`/`aria-label` is a markup change outside prep.
2. **Visibility by inline `style.display`, not by class or attribute**, on: `#audioMini`, `#audioSceneBox`, `#audioPlayRow` (js/20:7579–7612, 7463), `#mandalaPanel` (js/34:518), `.anim-lib-list` (js/20:3923, 3931), `#recKeepRow` (js/03:1119), `#audioTimingPanel` (js/40:1290, 1300), the sidebar `.atv-controls` host (js/40:1560–1561), `.atv-every` (js/40:1881). The theme cannot select these states without `[style*=…]`; adding `hidden`/`aria-hidden`/a class beside them is a script change. Note `#kaleidoPanel` uses `.open` + a stylesheet rule (05f:85; styles.css:1141–1152) while its sibling `#mandalaPanel` uses inline display + `.open` (34:516–520); `.collapsible` has no CSS at all (34:514 comment).
3. **State that lives only in JS variables**: Timing-Only's mapping suppression (`timingModeSnap`, js/20:7540–7554) has no DOM mark; the recorder's `recPendingTake` surfaces only as `#recKeepRow`'s display; "Everything"/"Local Only" and the length cap are `aria-pressed` (good) but the countdown digit is a `data-rec-count` on `#canvas-area`, outside the section.
4. **Rebuilt-from-scratch widgets**: `renderSlots` (js/20:3864) and `renderLib` (3939) empty and recreate their buttons on every library/playback event; `buildLaneRows` (js/40:1788) does the same for lane rows; `buildControls` (js/30:887) for scene controls. Any runtime property (`--i`, `--ga`) the theme sets on those nodes is lost on the next render; the theme must set them inside the render functions or on a surviving parent (`.anim-slot`, `.atv-lanes`).
5. **Travelling enable row.** B3–B6 move between `#audioMini`, `#audioSceneBox` and the body-level `#audioDrawerEnableHost` (js/20:7563–7573). Theme rules scoped to `#sidebar-right` will not reach them in Full mode; the existing CSS already styles them by own class (css/21-sidebar.css:1981–1994). Mapping (E) should treat the row as one component in two shells.
6. **Light-surface drawer.** `.rec-drawer` ships a light gradient and dark text by default with a dark variant block (css/styles.css:942–977). Which block is live depends on a media/class condition not read here; the theme doc should say what the drawer becomes.
7. **Registry vs DOM drift.** `scripts/test/inventory/params.json` lists `audioMode` options without "timing" and cites js/20 lines 3594–3597 / 3626–3662 / 4027–4071 that now sit at 7215–7223, 7243, 7716–7747, 7757–7767; `audioReactSource` is described as a dynamic device list but has three static options. `ParamRegistry.SELECTS.audioAutoSplatMode` (js/01a:295; also in js/06b:60 and js/12:127) has **no element in the DOM** — the pattern is seven `.ar-pattern-btn` buttons whose choice is never persisted (js/22:1478). Is the select a dead key or a missing control?
8. **Keyboard reach.** `.anim-slot-clear` is `opacity:0` until hover with no `:focus-visible` rule (css/21-sidebar.css:394–410); `.anim-lib-row` is a draggable `div` with click-to-play and no keyboard equivalent (js/20:3949–3976); `.atv-dot` is a swatch, not a control. A11y items for Gabriel, not styling.
9. **Drawer close vs Audio Mode.** `studioDrawer.close()` removes `.open` (js/28:38–41); whether it also resets `#audioMode` from "full" was not read (28:38–49). If not, the select says Full while nothing is open — a state the theme will show.
10. **Mandala rewrites shared controls.** Turning Mandala Overlay on (K1) drives `#kaleidoToggle`, `#kaleidoMode`, `#kaleidoSegments`, `#kAngle`, `#multiplier`, `#brushSize` and the four proxied sim sliders through their real inputs (js/34:66–70, 4xx–477), so a personal switch causes a burst of shared changes in a room; `exitMandala` restores them (488–512). Expected behaviour, but the "another player's glow" will light the whole Kaleido panel on a peer's screen.
11. **Body-level nodes relying on `[data-group]` hue**: `#recMini` (moved into the section, fine), `#recDrawer`, `#audioTimingPanel` (`dataset.group`, js/40:846) and `#recTakePrompt` (js/03:316) read `--sec-h/--sec-c` from `[data-group="expressive"]` (css/01-buttons.css:43–45). Cascade-layer step 1 must keep those selectors winning for `<body>` children.
12. **`data-no-scale="1"`** on `#audioSeek`, `#audioVolume`, `#audioKaleidoSpin`, the drawer's Lead/Nudge/Similarity sliders: read by script (which file was not traced here; workstream B should list it). Keep it.
13. **Section titles are a persistence key** (`sidebar.sections`, js/20:9205–9212): renaming "Animations", "Audio" or "Kaleidoscope" in a theme pass breaks collapse restore.
14. **Not verified in this slice**: whether any of the 41 `overlay:.controls` orphans duplicates an id from this scope; the exact Tunnel control rows beyond js/30:330–345; the 05h binding for registry sliders with `configKey:null`.

### A7 — Right sidebar 5: Text, Focus, Display

Also written to `prep/work/open-items/A7-right-5.md`.

1. **`--i` name collision with CSS prep step 5.** `stampGroupRows` (js/20:2657–2669) writes `--rows` and **`--i`** inline on every `.sidebar-section[data-group]`, and css/21-sidebar.css:181–190 reads `--i` as the section's row index for the title gradient. The theme's per-control brightness property is also named `--i`. Custom properties inherit, so every control inside a section already inherits an integer `--i` (0…N) today; any theme rule that reads `--i` on an element the theme did not set it on will get the row index. Decide: rename the theme property, rename the section one (a selector-contract change since 20 writes it), or scope the theme's reads. Report only.
2. **Selected text line has no DOM state.** The chosen row in `#textOverlayList` is marked only by inline `border`/`background` strings (js/20:6533–6535, 6577–6579); no `aria-selected`, `aria-current`, class or data attribute. Styling the selected row through the theme needs a hook, and adding one is a (small) script change. The rows are also `div`s without role or `tabIndex`, so the list is pointer-only (a11y baseline fact, not a prep change).
3. **Toggle buttons that only use `.active`.** Italic (20:6725–6729), the three Align buttons (20:6763–6767), Arrange on Canvas (20:6491), the four Focus formats (21:264–266, 285–288) and `#penWindowBtn` (47:1767) have no `aria-pressed`. Step 4 wants ARIA-state rules beside class rules; these have no ARIA state to key on without script. The hotkey picker (`aria-pressed`, 48:634) and Clear All (`aria-expanded`, 20:7035) already do.
4. **"Disabled" by inline opacity.** `syncBgEnabled` (20:6842–6850) dims the background colour, opacity, padding and corner controls with inline `opacity:0.35; pointer-events:none`, not `:disabled`/`aria-disabled`. A theme's disabled look cannot reach them through state selectors.
5. **Inline `cssText` throughout Text.** Nearly every element of the Text section is styled by inline strings in js/20 (list in the section notes). Step 2's tokens cannot replace values inside `style=""` attributes without touching js/20; decide whether Text stays "legacy, inline" for the first theme pass or whether adding classes (markup-only, no script reads them) is allowed there.
6. **Unnamed or title-named selects in Text.** `#textOverlayFont` has an empty accessible name (its caption is a plain `div`, 20:6347–6358); `#textOverlayWeight`, `#textOverlayCase`, `#textOverlayColliderMode` and `#textOverlayHotkeyAction` are named only by their `title`, two of them by a long sentence. "Accessibility only goes up" means this is a baseline fact for F3, not something prep fixes; flagged so the a11y baseline expects it.
7. **Names that include a key cap or a value.** `#windowMode` reads "WINDOW MODE F11" and `#canvasOpacity` "CANVAS OPACITY (SIM VISIBILITY) 100%" because js/17 puts the cap inside the `<label>` (17:120, 152) and the readout span lives there too. The slider's accessible name changes whenever its value does. F3's "every row still true" check needs to compare names loosely for these, or the theme must not move the cap/readout out of the label (that would change the name — a behaviour-adjacent change).
8. **Electron-only and permission-gated rows are invisible to the web baseline.** `#penWindowMouseReturn` is removed from the DOM on the web build (47:1800–1804), `#transparentRestartRow` stays `hidden` without Electron (05h:63), the `.pen-screen` radios appear only after `getScreenDetails` permission or on Electron (47:577–640), and `.bg-transparency-group` only under Transparent Background (css/styles.css:212). F1's screenshot set will not cover these states unless it drives Electron or toggles `#transparentMode` first (which persists immediately — fine in the scratch profile).
9. **`#streamFormatLock` has no handler.** Nothing runs when it is toggled; `applyFormat` reads it live (21:228). For step 5 the only code path is the native `change` event.
10. **Where a remote value lands for the shared Display controls.** `#canvasOpacity` and `#captureDimming` arrive through `glideWrite` (06c:101–110: `input` per frame, `change` on landing) and `#backgroundColorPicker` through `setVal(…, 'input')` (12:1136) under `window.__mpApplyingRemote` (06b:263). Confirm those two call sites are where the theme should set `--ga/--gc/--pc`; there is no other hook that knows the change was remote.
11. **`body.photosafe-on` has no CSS rule.** 05e:197/221 toggles it as the "DOM-side guard" for canvas background/opacity transitions, but no stylesheet matches it today. If the theme adds transitions on `#canvas-area` or `#canvas`, this is the class PhotoSafe expects to gate them with. Report only.
12. **Soft contracts on section titles.** Collapse state (`sidebar.sections`, 20:9204–9208), js/43 hide keys (`section:Text` …) and js/44 recipe targets all key on the title text; the "Format" heading label and the captions in Text are free text. The merge agent should list the three titles with the other soft contracts.
13. **Simple layout hides all three sections** (43:49–58). Baselines run in the Everything layout (scratch profile); a Simple-layout baseline would show none of this slice.
14. **PhotoSafe persistence is trusted-event only** (05e:208–218). Baseline scripts that dispatch `change` on `#photoSafeToggle` change the session but not storage, and only a trusted click reaches the off-confirmation alertdialog — screenshots of that dialog need `Input.dispatchMouseEvent`.
15. **`#lockCanvasBorders` markup is orphaned in index.html** (1332–1335, inside the Light Shift block); harmless, but workstream B should not read its neighbours as Display controls.
16. **The dump counted two non-controls in scope**: `#textOverlayClearAsk` (`role=group`, [539]) and the "Format" `label.brush-section-label` with an empty name ([546]). Both are listed above so the critic's diff reconciles.

### A8 — Right sidebar 6: Export, Settings, quality underbar #quality-underbar

1. **`statsToggle` ("Debug Info") is not in Settings although `buildSettingsSection` moves it** (js/20:8401 `moveCheckboxGroup('statsToggle', body)`). The dump places it in **Display** (order 582). Cause: `buildDisplaySection` (js/20:4583) moved the node into the still-detached right sidebar first, so `document.getElementById('statsToggle')` returns null when Settings builds (the detached-tree trap js/20 documents at 8995-9001). The line is dead code, behaviour-neutral; inventory ownership = Display slice. Flag for the merge agent so neither slice drops it, and for Gabriel whether the dead move should go (outside prep).
2. **Inventory of record gaps.** (a) `shadeFormResolution` is in the registry (js/01a:267) and the underbar but missing from `scripts/test/inventory/params.json` (111 entries). (b) The registry lists 6 options for `visualResolution` and 10 for `physicsResolution` (js/01a:260-261) while the live selects have 8 and 12 (the dump's `options` 8/12, text "8192 6144 4096 …" / "4096 3072 2048 …"): index.html adds high tiers the registry does not know. Report only.
3. **Interface checkboxes have no ids and key on `data-ui-key`** (js/43:426-428, 468). That attribute is a script contract (README session notes), so the mapping must style these rows through `.ui-vis-row`, `:checked`, `:disabled` and `.is-pinned`, never through the data attribute. Also: pinned "Settings" is `disabled` with no `aria-disabled`; the row's accessible name concatenates "Settings" + "always shown" ("Settingsalways shown" in the dump) because the hint span sits inside the label.
4. **Simple | Everything buttons carry state as `.active` only** (js/43:455-457), no `aria-pressed`/`aria-checked`; same for the Saved-preset rows (`.user-preset-btn.active`, js/20:8546) and the running Quick Export buttons (`.btn--record.active`, js/24:1846-1847). CSS prep step 4 wants an ARIA hook beside the class; adding `aria-pressed` would be a script change. Report only.
5. **Inline `style.cssText` in JS carries most of the Export and Settings styling**: Export inputs/selects/labels (js/20:8027, 8035, 8042, 8070, 8077, 8137, 8143, 8178, 8189-8190, 8203, 8217, 8229-8233, 8251-8252, 8283, 8292), the preset and ComfyUI blocks (8406, 8409, 8432, 8435, 8440, 8448, 8468, 8483, 8486, 8489, 8495, 8497), `#exportStatus`/progress colours (`#58a6ff`, `#3fb950`), and `showPresetStatus` colours (js/12:2199, 2201, 2203, 2234). The CSS audit (C) tokenises stylesheets; these values live in JS, so step 2 cannot reach them without a JS edit. Needs a decision on whether inline JS styles are in scope for the style-only reskin.
6. **Electron-only blocks are invisible to the headless baseline**: Export → Output Folder (js/20:8268-8307, `typeof require`), Settings → ComfyUI (js/20:8514, `window.comfyuiBridge`), `#openPresetsFolderBtn` shown (js/12b:514-520). F1/F3 need an Electron capture or an explicit exclusion.
7. **Underbar extras re-parent between `#quality-underbar` and `<body>`** when the bar is hidden (slowdown js/08a:525-528, clock/label js/52:151-152) and take `.floating`. Theme rules scoped `#quality-underbar > …` miss the floating copies; `.floating` is the hook. Also the bar itself is deliberately chrome-less (`background:none; pointer-events:none`, css/20-mixer-strip.css:28-35) and js/01-config.js:1402-1411 measures its band for the canvas fit: a themed plate must keep `pointer-events:none` on the bar and not grow its height, or the canvas fit changes.
8. **States not reachable in a static baseline**: Quick Export running (`■ Stop and save`, `.btn--record`, siblings `disabled`, `#exportStopBtn` shown, `body.export-capturing`, `#canvas-area[data-export-clock]`, js/24:1834-1853, 1880-1906); the slowdown button (needs a measured fps drop); Saved-preset rows (scratch profile has none); hotkey rows (none saved); `#resetAppHint` (seeded away); the open `.qub-dd` list (`.open`, `aria-expanded=true`, `.qub-dd-opt.sel`). F1 should script the ones it can (open a pill; save one preset and one hotkey on the scratch profile) and list the rest as unbaselined.
9. **Section titles are a persistence and mount contract**: `sidebar.sections` is keyed by title text (js/20:9201-9210), js/43/49/58 mount into Settings by title (43:350-358, 49:133-141, 58:853-858), and Interface keys are `section:<title>` (43:160). Renaming "Export" or "Settings" in the theme (e.g. via `text-transform` is fine; changing the DOM text is not) breaks all three.
10. **Section title button has no `aria-controls`** to its `.section-body` (js/20:9324-9327), and the Export number/select fields (`#exportVideoDuration`, `#exportVideoFPS`, `#exportGifDuration`, `#exportGifFPS`) have no `label[for]`, so their AX names are empty in the dump (orders 600, 602, 605, 607). Accessibility must not go down; fixing it would be a markup change, so report only.
11. **Native `<select>`s in Export** (`#exportGround`, `#exportVideoFPS`, `#exportGifFPS`) keep the OS option popup that the underbar replaced with `makeQubDropdown` because it "can't be dark-themed" (js/20:2679-2683). The theme cannot style their popups either; decide whether they stay native.
12. **`.qub-dd-opt.sel` is the only `var(--accent)` use in this slice** (css/20-mixer-strip.css:202) and the list body is hard-coded `#23262b` (162-176); the pill text uses `--sec-h` from `:root` through `btn--ghost` (css/01-buttons.css:217-236). Noted for the token worklist (C) and the mapping (E).
13. **js/50-look-links.js and js/48-hotkeys.js were in this slice's reading list but own no Export/Settings DOM** (50's Copy-link button is in the Presets panel, 50:1098-1109; 48 only supplies `picker()` to 49/58). Confirm the top-bar slice lists `.look-link-copy`.

### A9 — Everything else: titlebar, canvas overlays, hotkey sheet, radial menu, popovers, modals, toasts, pen launcher

Also written to `prep/work/open-items/A9-elsewhere.md`.

1. **Two layer-handle systems.** js/26-layer-transform.js:4-10 says it "replaces the old in-DOM handle system", but js/05l-layers-transform.js:413, 443-479 still builds `.layer-resize-handle` / `.layer-rotate-handle` (with a "🔄" glyph) on the active layer, and css/styles.css:383, 411 still style them. Which one ships? Styling both is wasted work; removing one is a behaviour change. Report only.
2. **`#customCursor`** (index.html:568; js/05g-arm-colors.js:745 fades it; css/styles.css:331) sits beside js/31's `#brushCursor`. Does it still draw anything, or is it dead markup like the `.controls` orphans?
3. **Orphaned static `.controls` panel** (index.html:633+, 41 dump records under `overlay:.controls`, `body > .controls { display: none }` css/20-mixer-strip.css:221). Ids inside it are still read: `saveAsPresetBtn`, `userPreset*`, `shootingStarToggle`, `ss*` by js/12-save-load.js and js/04d; `jellyfishBtn` by js/04c; `portalBtn` by js/04e; `chimeraBtn` by no `getElementById` — but its inline `onclick="playChimeraAnimation()"` (index.html:1494) → `window.playChimeraAnimation` js/04e:836 is live, as is the id-less Portrait button's `onclick="playPortraitAnimation()"` (index.html:1489) → js/04e:447 (corrected after the critique). All fifteen interactive orphans are listed by id in §32. Confirms F0's open item 1: dead on screen, alive to script. Not counted as controls here.
4. **Brush ghost settings live in `localStorage`, not settingsManager** (`ui.brushCursorGhost`, `ui.brushCursorOutlineOpacity`, js/31:184-185). Settings → Clear and the reset do not touch them, and they are outside the registry, so a theme that wants to read "ghost on" has no DOM attribute, only `#brushGhostToggle.checked` and `style.display` on `#brushCursorGhostClip`.
5. **State only knowable from JS variables, no DOM attribute** (CSS prep step 5 needs a hook): radial menu open (`st`, js/58; the root only toggles `hidden`); zoom view scale and Zoom Mode (`#zoomBadge` / `#zoomModeFrame` are driven by inline `style` from js/35:159-175, no class); colour-picker sampling (`body.cp-sampling` exists, good) but the loupe's "pressing" state is text only; the maximize state of `#max-button` (no `aria-pressed`, no label change, js/00:117-126); the Debug panel's enabled state is `style.display` only.
6. **Inline `cssText` surfaces that a theme token cannot reach:** `#brushCursor` and its five siblings (js/31:38-78, 193-213), `#kaleidoGuides` / `#mandalaGuides` / `#strokeLockGuide` (hard-coded `rgba` lines), `#zoomBadge` / `#zoomModeFrame` (js/35, reads `var(--accent)` at least), `#samDownloadModal` and `#depthDownloadModal` (whole modal inline, plus an injected unlayered `#samModalStyles` keyframes sheet js/16:265-279), five toasts (`#look-toast`, `#export-toast`, `#gestureIndicator`, `#pasteIndicator`, the first-run hint), the file-drop hover sheet (js/32:75-81, `!important`), `#text-overlay-container` / `#text-arrange-canvas` (js/23:204, 783), `.splash-version` and `#photoWarn`'s own inline `<style>` (index.html:249-301, which `auditButtons()` exempts). Restyling any of these needs a JS edit (moving the values to a class), which is outside "style only"; decision needed on which ones the theme covers.
7. **Dialogs without `role` in markup**, relying on `DialogFocus.hold` to add it at first open: `#hotkeyOverlay` (index.html:1609), `#deletePaletteModal` (index.html:1991; uses no hold at all), `#appCloseModal` (js/04f:647), `#appConfirmModal` (js/20:365), `#maskEditorOverlay` (js/15:144), `#recDrawer` (no role, not a dialog). Baseline a11y should record the pre-open and post-open states separately; styling through `[role=dialog]` would miss them before first open.
8. **Menus whose items are plain buttons.** `.arm-tip-menu[role=menu]` holds `role=button` options with no `menuitem` role and no arrow-key handling (js/20:712-790 does not call `DialogFocus.menu`; the preset `⋯` menu and the shape menu do). Styling via `[role=menuitem]` would treat the two menus differently. `#mpRoomMenu` is correct (`menuitemcheckbox` / `menuitem`).
9. **`.mask-tool-tabs[role=tablist]`** holds `button.mask-tool-tab` with no `role=tab` / `aria-selected` (js/15 template ~line 210); `.studio-tab` in the drawer has no tab semantics at all. ARIA-state styling (`[aria-selected]`) has nothing to bind to today.
10. **Unnamed or title-named controls:** the cue editor's Similarity, Lead and Nudge ranges have no id and (Lead / Nudge) no `for`-linked label (dump `o` 754, 763, 765 name ""); `canvas.ace-canvas[aria-label=""]` (`o` 757); `.cp-num` fields are named by `title` only (`o` 7, 9, 11); `#statsCloseBtn`, `#studioCloseBtn` and the titlebar buttons rely on `title`. Accessibility "only goes up", so these are baseline facts, not changes.
11. **Pointer-only chrome with no keyboard path:** the eight canvas `.resize-handle`s, the eight `#captureArea .cap-rz` grips, `.pfi-marker` drags, the `.cp-swatch-old` revert half, `#zoomBadge` (a clickable `div`). Styling `:focus-visible` on them is moot; flagged in case the theme adds visible affordances.
12. **`#mpRoomToast` fades with an opacity transition** (js/06e:462-464, css/21-sidebar.css:1497) while every other overlay in this slice avoids transitions over the canvas for the Electron compositor reason (js/58:71-72, js/21:49, js/32:236-237, js/49:764-768). Not a styling question, but the theme's motion rules should not add more.
13. **`.rec-close`** (`#studioCloseBtn`) matched no stylesheet selector in css/*.css (the only hits were for `.rec-close` inside other names); it is styled by the generic `button` rules. Confirm with workstream C before giving it a themed rule.
14. **Ownership overlaps** the merge agent must dedupe: `#mpInvitePop` / `#mpRoomMenu` (Swirl Together slice), the colour picker's inline variant in `#palettePickerHost` and the `.pfi` panel (Colors slice), the Audio drawer's contents (Audio slice), `#penWindowBtn` and the Debug Info toggle `#statsToggle` (Display / Settings slice), `#slowdownCell` and `#resetAppBtn` (underbar), the Settings → Radial Menu / Hotkeys blocks and `#hkBindBar` (Settings slice). This file lists them once each; the merge should pick one home.
15. **Governor toggles in the Debug panel** (`#governorToggle`, `#governorAllowRes`, `#governorAllowFx`, `#idleVramToggle`) are not in the registry and not in `MP_PERF_LOCAL_KEYS`; they are plainly not part of a look, but the "Syncs?" column cannot cite a line that says so. Confirm with js/08a's own persistence (js/08a:87-89, 734-736).
16. **`#mandalaGuides` lacks `aria-hidden`** (js/34:244-248) while `#kaleidoGuides` and `#strokeLockGuide` have it. A one-attribute a11y fix, outside prep.
17. **Lazy surfaces not in any dump** and therefore not pixel-baselined by F1 unless F1 opens them: the startup fork, every `.delete-modal` except delete-palette, the SAM / depth modals, the mask editor, the layer-transform overlay, text arrange, the take prompt, `#captureArea`, the preset `⋯` menu, the eyedropper veil / loupe, `#hkBindBar`, `#hkWheel`, the radial note and the toasts. F1's state list should name which of these it captures.
